### [91] [It's Not Just More Demos: Counterfactual Action Sensitivity Coverage for Data-Efficient Robust Robot Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27261)
*Giovanni D'urso,Kaushik Roy,Nicholas Lawrance,Brendan Tidd*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CFNBC（反事实干扰行为克隆）框架，用于离线数据选择以实现针对性鲁棒性修复。该方法通过测量策略在成对干净/干扰观测下的"动作漂移"来识别策略特定的敏感性信号，从而从候选池中挑选紧凑且响应多样的修复数据集，无需在线执行或成功标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动模仿学习策略对视觉干扰（光照、干扰物、颜色变化等）极其脆弱，即使训练数据多样化也难以确定哪些额外演示对当前策略最有信息量，缺乏针对性的数据选择方法。

Method: 提出CFNBC框架：1）从干净演示训练名义策略；2）生成成对的干净与干扰观测（保持专家动作不变）；3）计算动作漂移（策略在干扰下的预测动作变化）作为策略特定的敏感性信号；4）基于该信号从候选池中选择响应多样的紧凑修复集，无需rollout成功标签或在线执行。

Result: 在MuJoCo双臂立方体转移和SimplerEnv立方体堆叠任务中，动作漂移与干扰诱导的失败相关；响应引导修复仅用20-30个精选候选即显著优于相同预算的随机选择，并接近更大随机修复预算的性能。

Conclusion: 支持数据中心的鲁棒性修复观点：最有用的数据不是数量最多、视觉最多样或明显困难的，而是覆盖当前策略脆弱响应模式的那些示例。

Abstract: Visuomotor imitation learning has demonstrated success for manipulation tasks. However, the trained policies remain brittle to visual `nuisances', with even minor task-preserving variations such as lighting, distractions or changes in colour result in heavy degradation of the trained policy's performance. While increasing data diversity can improve robustness, it is unclear which additional demonstrations are informative for a particular trained policy. We propose Counterfactual Nuisance Behaviour Cloning (CFNBC), an offline data-selection framework for targeted robustness repair. Starting from a nominal policy trained on `clean' demonstrations, CFNBC generates paired clean and nuisance observations that preserve the expert action, then measures \emph{action drift}: the change in the policy's predicted action under a nuisance that should not alter the desired behaviour. This provides a policy-specific sensitivity signal for selecting a compact, response-diverse repair set from a larger candidate pool, without requiring rollout success labels or online policy execution. We show in MuJoCo bimanual cube transfer and SimplerEnv cube stacking that action drift correlates with nuisance-induced failure, and that response-guided repair with only $20$--$30$ selected candidates substantially outperforms matched-budget random selection while approaching the performance of much larger random repair budgets. These results support a data-centric view of robustness repair: the most useful data are not necessarily the most numerous, visually diverse, or obviously difficult, but the examples that cover fragile response modes of the current policy.

</details>


### [92] [Simulation of Surgical Suturing Using Position-Based Dynamics and the Material Point Method for Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27494)
*Tleukhan Mussin,Yafei Ou,Mahdi Tavakoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的手术缝合仿真环境，采用基于位置动力学（PBD）的缝线和基于物质点法（MPM）的软组织建模，实现了两者间的双向接触耦合（含摩擦力和阻力），并在GPU上优化并行执行。基于ML-Agents训练的强化学习智能体在针插入和针拔出子任务中分别达到80%和68%的成功率（在最严格距离阈值下）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人手术研究需要高性能仿真器，而手术缝合仿真面临建模复杂异质物体（刚性器械、软组织、流体）的独特挑战。现有研究大多独立模拟缝线或软组织，很少考虑完整的软组织缝合场景（包括缝合针插入时缝线与可变形组织间的接触）。

Method: 采用PBD建模缝线、MPM建模软组织，引入PBD缝线与MPM软组织之间的接触耦合法，考虑双向接触并包含摩擦力和阻力；仿真器针对GPU执行优化，使用多个CUDA流实现并行场景；构建基于ML-Agents的强化学习环境，训练自主缝合子任务（针插入、驱动、拔出）。

Result: 强化学习智能体在仿真器中表现出稳定的学习过程，在最严格的距离阈值下，针插入成功率为80%，针拔出成功率为68%。

Conclusion: 该仿真环境能够实现视觉上可信的缝线-组织交互，并为自主缝合子任务提供了可训练的强化学习平台，验证了PBD-MPM耦合方法在手术缝合仿真中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in robotics research have created a strong demand for high-performance simulators. Surgical robotics simulation faces unique challenges due to the need to model diverse objects, such as rigid instruments, soft tissue, and fluids. While many studies simulate sutures or soft tissue independently, only a few have considered the complete soft-tissue suturing scenario, including the contact between sutures and deformable tissue during suture insertion. Building on previous work, this paper presents a novel suturing simulation environment using sutures modelled by position-based dynamics (PBD) and soft bodies modelled by the material point method (MPM) while considering two-way contact with frictional and drag forces. We introduce a contact coupling method between the PBD suture and the MPM soft tissue, enabling visually plausible suture-tissue interactions. The simulator is optimized for GPU execution with parallel scenes using multiple CUDA streams, and we present a Reinforcement Learning (RL) environment for autonomous suturing sub-tasks, including needle insertion, driving, and extraction. Using ML-Agents, RL agents trained in the simulator show stable learning and achieve 80% and 68% success rates in needle insertion and extraction, respectively, under the strictest distance threshold.

</details>


### [93] [Corrigible Assistance in One Round: Pragmatic-Pedagogic Best Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27508)
*Elle Lazarski,Jaime Fernández Fisac*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究人类-机器人协作中的辅助博弈（assistance games），识别出一类可通过务实-教学推理（pragmatic-pedagogic reasoning）在单时间步内消除目标不确定性的博弈类别，使其能够通过可处理的best-response过程精确求解，并证明主流逆最优控制在推理上限上存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助博弈中，机器人需要在信息不对称（人类知道目标，机器人需从观测和交互中推断）情况下制定最优策略。然而，一般情况下精确求解此类博弈需要规划POMDP，在线计算不可行。作者希望找到一类特殊结构使得问题可精确且高效求解。

Method: 作者识别出一类辅助博弈，其中务实-教学推理（pragmatic-pedagogic reasoning）能够在单个时间步内消除目标不确定性，从而将全时域博弈转化为可通过可计算的best-response过程精确求解的问题。作者将此方法与主流逆最优控制方法进行了对比分析，并通过协作积木搭建示例进行验证。

Result: 研究表明，在这类辅助博弈中，主流逆最优控制方法存在推理天花板（inference ceiling），阻碍了人机对齐；而务实-教学推理能够通过采取在纯任务执行视角下看似等价但具有沟通效应的动作，立即消除目标歧义，突破这一障碍。理论结果和所提方法在协作积木搭建示例上得到了验证。

Conclusion: 本文为一类重要的辅助博弈提供了精确可解的框架，揭示了务实-教学推理相对于逆最优控制在目标推断与对齐方面的优势，为该类问题提供了理论保障和实用解法。

Abstract: Assistance games formalize human-robot collaboration under asymmetric information: the human knows the goal, while the robot must infer it from observation and interaction in order to assist effectively. In general, computing optimal assistance game strategies online is intractable, since exact solutions require planning in a POMDP. We identify a class of assistance games in which pragmatic-pedagogic reasoning resolves goal uncertainty in a single time step, rendering the full-horizon game exactly solvable by a tractable best-response procedure. Within this class, we show that mainstream inverse optimal control exhibits an inference ceiling that hinders alignment, while pragmatic-pedagogic reasoning overcomes this barrier by immediately disambiguating goals through actions that look equivalent under task execution alone. Finally, we validate our theoretical results and proposed method on a simple collaborative block-building example.

</details>


### [94] [Failure Detection for Surgical Robot Imitation Policies via Flow-Matching World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27511)
*Zhefeng Huang,Yilin Cai,Ankit Patel,Mohammad Hajiha,Brendan Browne,Yue Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FoMo-FD，一种基于流匹配世界模型的机器人手术失败检测方法。它通过学习名义短视距视觉动力学，利用逆传输非一致性评分在窗口级别检测视觉-动作不一致，无需失败演示数据，通过保形校准获得任务特定的检测阈值。在dVRK平台的四个手术操作任务、二十种失败模式上验证，达到96.6%的成功检测率（FDR）和仅1.3%的误报率（FAR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人虽然通过模仿学习展现出巨大潜力，但其安全部署仍面临重大挑战。由于手术任务具有安全关键性、环境复杂多变、失败数据稀缺、操作动力学高度可变，以及需要在漏检和误报之间取得平衡，传统失败检测方法的开发十分困难。作者旨在解决上述挑战，提出无需失败样本即可实现可靠失败检测的方法。

Method: FoMo-FD采用动作条件化的流匹配（flow-matching）世界模型学习名义上的短视距视觉动力学。方法包含三个核心要素：(1) 使用流匹配世界模型预测短期内的视觉状态演变；(2) 对观测到的终点潜在表示（endpoint latents）计算逆传输（inverse-transport）非一致性评分，以检测视觉-动作不一致；(3) 通过保形校准（conformal calibration）在成功执行数据上获得任务特定的检测阈值，无需假设未来的失败类型。

Result: 在da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK)平台上，对四个手术相关操作任务和二十种失败模式进行了仿真与真实实验评估。结果显示FoMo-FD优于基于观测级别的异常基线和同一世界模型的预测误差变体；腕部相机视角表现最佳，达到96.6%的失败检测率（FDR）和1.3%的误报率（FAR）。

Conclusion: FoMo-FD通过流匹配世界模型与保形校准相结合，提供了一种无需失败演示即可实现高准确率失败检测的解决方案，在手术机器人安全部署方面具有实际应用价值。方法在仿真和真实实验中均展现出优于现有基线的性能，验证了窗口级视觉-动作一致性检测的有效性。

Abstract: Imitation learning has shown increasing promise for autonomous robotic surgery, yet safe deployment remains challenging due to the safety-critical nature of surgical tasks and the complexity and variability of surgical environments. Failure detection is therefore an essential safeguard, but its development remains difficult due to the challenges of scarce failure data, highly variable manipulation dynamics, and the need to balance missed detections against disruptive false alarms. To address these challenges, we introduce FoMo-FD (Flow-Matching World Model for Failure Detection), a failure detection method that learns nominal short-horizon visual dynamics with an action-conditioned flow-matching world model. FoMo-FD scores the inverse-transport nonconformity of observed endpoint latents, enabling window-level detection of visual-action inconsistencies without requiring failure demonstrations. Detection thresholds are obtained by conformal calibration on successful executions, yielding task-specific alarms without assuming future failure types. We evaluate FoMo-FD on four surgically relevant manipulation tasks with twenty failure modes across simulation and real-world experiments using the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK). Results show that FoMo-FD outperforms observation-level anomaly baselines and a prediction-error variant of the same world model, with the wrist-camera view achieving the strongest performance, including a 96.6% failure detection rate (FDR) at a 1.3% false alarm rate (FAR).

</details>


### [95] [Cross-Embodiment Transfer via Behavior-Aligned Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27549)
*Ajay Sridhar,Jensen Gao,Jonathan Yang,Jean Mercat,Suneel Belkhale,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中使用行为对齐表示（如物体边界框、语言动作、末端执行器轨迹）来促进跨机器人形态的迁移学习。通过构建基于仿真的基准测试，作者发现末端执行器轨迹对迁移特别有益，表示在大规模预训练数据下更有用，且能利用无动作数据。该方法将仿真到真实世界的跨形态迁移的任务完成进度提升了28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模模仿学习在机器人操作领域取得了显著进展，部分得益于利用多种机器人形态的数据集。然而，实现显著的跨形态迁移仍然具有挑战性。作者希望通过引入行为对齐表示来统一大规模跨形态数据，从而增强不同机器人形态之间的迁移能力。

Method: 作者提出在VLA模型中引入行为对齐表示（包括物体边界框、语言运动、末端执行器轨迹），并构建了一个基于仿真的基准测试来评估跨形态迁移。该基准测试使用多样的跨形态数据训练，评估向新形态的迁移性能。作者比较了不同表示类型及其整合方式，并验证了这些表示在仿真到真实世界迁移中的效果。

Result: 研究表明：（1）末端执行器轨迹对跨形态迁移特别有益；（2）表示在更大规模的预训练数据集下更有用；（3）表示可以用于利用无动作数据进行训练；（4）该方法能增强仿真到真实的跨形态迁移，使基于仿真数据预训练的真实机器人策略的任务完成进度提升28%。

Conclusion: 行为对齐表示（尤其是末端执行器轨迹）能够作为跨形态VLA模型的统一信号，有效促进跨形态迁移，并能在数据和动作范式上带来额外收益，包括利用无动作数据和增强仿真到真实的迁移能力。

Abstract: Recent progress in large-scale imitation learning for robot manipulation has been driven by leveraging datasets across a wide range of robot embodiments. However, achieving significant cross-embodiment transfer is often still challenging. In this work, we study the role of using behavior-aligned representations (e.g., object bounding boxes, language motions, end-effector traces of robot motion) in vision-language-action (VLA) models to promote cross-embodiment transfer. We hypothesize that by possessing invariances across embodiments while being predictive of robot actions, these representations can help unify large-scale cross-embodiment data to enhance transfer. To assess our hypothesis, we develop a simulation-based benchmark designed to assess transfer with diverse cross-embodiment data to new embodiments. Using this benchmark, we compare different representations and ways of incorporating them. We identify that end-effector traces can be particularly beneficial for transfer, representations are generally more useful with larger prior datasets, and can be used to benefit from action-free data. We also demonstrate that they can enhance sim-to-real cross-embodiment transfer, improving task completion progress of real robot policies pre-trained on simulation data by 28%. We provide videos of our evaluations at our website: https://ajaysridhar.com/barx/.

</details>


### [96] [A Systems Engineering Framework for Vision-Language-Enabled UAV Triage and Disaster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27597)
*Swapnil Saha,Bhuvan Rajanasiriyur Jagadeesha,Karishma Patnaik,Neelakshi Majumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探讨将视觉语言模型（VLM）嵌入人类-UAV回路中作为协调代理，而非仅作为决策支持工具，通过MBSE方法构建架构，并在集成仿真环境中实现了三个核心组件，初步人因评估显示感知工作负荷降低、AI信任度和沟通清晰度评分较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM在灾害响应中的应用（如社交媒体监控、行动计划生成、警报翻译）仅限于决策支持角色，这会增加操作员负担，因为人类仍需将输出转化为跨团队和机器人资产的协调行动。亟需探索VLM作为协调代理嵌入人类-UAV回路中的可行性。

Method: 采用Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE)方法设计框架，用用例图和块定义图表示系统角色、内部结构和组件交互；实现三个关键组件（VLM协调代理、UAV任务控制、任务分配器）于集成仿真与控制环境中；开展七名参与者的人因评估。

Result: 初步人因评估显示：感知工作负荷在心理需求、努力和挫败感方面显著降低；AI信任度和沟通清晰度获得高评分；VLM能够有效促进操作员、任务控制逻辑和UAV任务执行之间的通信。

Conclusion: 该工作通过整合MBSE、软件在环测试和人因评估，推进了高风险灾害响应场景中可扩展的人类-自主系统团队协作，对航空航天自主性和民事安全具有更广泛的意义。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision Language Models (VLMs) have created new opportunities for disaster response, where responders must interpret large volumes of sensor data under time pressure. Current VLM applications include social media monitoring for situational awareness, generation of draft action plans, and translation of technical alerts into public-facing messages. While these efforts can accelerate information flow, they remain largely limited to decision-support roles. Such approaches can increase operator burden because humans must still translate outputs into coordinated actions across teams and robotic assets. This study explores the viability of embedding VLMs as coordination agents within the human-UAV loop. The proposed architecture integrates natural language interaction, mission-level task coordination, software-in-the-loop implementation, and communication aligned with the Incident Command System (ICS). Rather than functioning solely as advisory tools, VLMs facilitate communication between human operators, mission control logic, and UAV task execution. The framework was developed using a Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) approach, with use case and block definition diagrams representing system roles, internal structure, and component interactions. Three key elements, the VLM Coordinator Agent, UAV Mission Control, and Task Allocator, were implemented within an integrated simulation and control environment. A preliminary human-factors evaluation with seven participants showed reduced perceived workload across mental demand, effort, and frustration, along with high ratings for AI trust and communication clarity. By integrating MBSE, software-in-the-loop testing, and human-factors evaluation, this work advances scalable human-autonomy teaming for high-stakes disaster response, with broader implications for aerospace autonomy and civil safety.

</details>


### [97] [Arm2Air: Cross-Embodiment Skeleton Transfer for 3D Relay Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27627)
*Dohun Lee,Kyeonghyun Yoo,Seokmin Kim,Byongho Lee,Seungjoo Oh,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Arm2Air，通过跨具身迁移将机器人手臂的避障运动骨架迁移到无人机中继部署任务中。利用预训练的Neural MP模型生成有序骨架来预训练基于Transformer的迁移平台，再通过LoRA和少量目标域数据适配到无人机域。在密集三维城市地图上，该方法的规划运行时间、瓶颈容量、跳距方差和参数量均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机中继网络可在通信基础设施受损后恢复连接，但在城市环境中，中继部署需同时考虑视距遮挡、通信范围、飞行高度和三维障碍物，规划过程计算复杂且数据稀缺。现有方法缺乏高效的结构化先验利用，需要在数据效率与计算效率上取得突破。

Method: Arm2Air采用跨具身迁移框架：源域中利用预训练的Neural MP模型生成机器人手臂避障运动轨迹，将其转换为有序骨架序列，用于预训练基于Transformer的迁移平台；随后通过少量目标域数据和Low-Rank Adaptation（LoRA）将该平台适配到无人机中继部署任务。迁移得到的骨架用于初始化中继链，并针对连通性、瓶颈容量、时延和移动成本进行优化精炼。

Result: 在9张高杂波三维城市地图上，Arm2Air的中位端到端规划运行时间比最快的传统规划器降低64.9%。在30张密集城市地图的高遮挡分组中，相比IMPC-MD，瓶颈容量提升32.6%，容量方差降低74.7%，最大跳距降低13.2%，跳距方差降低75.2%，中继位移减少16.9%。仅用3张目标域训练地图，相比从零训练，中继位置均方根误差降低53.6%，仅更新0.134百万参数（对比Scratch和Full Fine-tuning的1.383百万）。

Conclusion: Arm2Air展示了计算和样本双高效的中继部署能力，并提出了一个更广泛的原则：跨异构具身任务迁移有序结构先验是可行且有价值的。该方法为无人机中继网络在城市场景中的快速部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) relay networks can restore connectivity after communication infrastructure is damaged. Urban relay placement is difficult because line-of-sight blockage, communication range, altitude, and three-dimensional obstacles must be considered jointly. Arm2Air transfers obstacle-avoidance skeletons from robot arms to UAV relay placement through cross-embodiment transfer. Source-domain robot-arm motions from a pretrained Neural MP model are converted into ordered skeletons that pretrain a transformer-based transfer platform, which is then adapted to the UAV domain using limited target data and Low-Rank Adaptation. The transferred skeleton initializes a relay chain that is refined for connectivity, bottleneck capacity, delay, and movement cost. On nine held-out high-clutter 3D urban maps, Arm2Air reduced median end-to-end planning runtime by 64.9 percent relative to the fastest conventional planner. On the high-obstruction group of a separate 30-map dense urban holdout, it increased bottleneck capacity by 32.6 percent, reduced capacity variance by 74.7 percent, reduced maximum hop distance by 13.2 percent, reduced hop-distance variance by 75.2 percent, and reduced relay displacement by 16.9 percent relative to IMPC-MD. With only three target-domain training maps, Arm2Air reduced relay-position root mean square error by 53.6 percent relative to training from scratch while updating 0.134 million parameters, compared with 1.383 million for Scratch and Full Fine-tuning. These results demonstrate computationally and data-efficient UAV relay placement and suggest a broader principle for transferring ordered structural priors across heterogeneous embodied tasks.

</details>


### [98] [LabEvolver: Training-Free Experience Evolution for Safe and Grounded Wet-Lab Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27690)
*Jingya Wang,Yuyang Gao,Liuzhenghao Lv,Yonghong Tian,Yuyang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LabEvolver是一个无需训练的框架，为湿实验室智能体赋予基于执行经验的"情景记忆"。它通过状态锚定的内部试错循环实现自适应感知、在线规划与安全验证，并通过外部演化循环将完成的轨迹蒸馏为可复用的技能、策略和安全经验。在机器人溶液配制任务中，pH调节完成时间降低48.2%，安全门拦截减少60.0%；在ALFWorld基准上，20步内累计成功率从ReAct的76.2%提升至91.4%（500项连续任务），展示了超越湿实验室场景的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有湿实验室智能体缺乏从实际执行中积累经验的能力，每次任务都从零开始规划，导致效率低下且存在安全隐患。作者希望构建一个无需训练的框架，让智能体能够"边做边学"，将执行轨迹转化为可复用的经验知识，从而提升任务完成效率与安全性，并探索闭环自动化科学发现的可能性。

Method: LabEvolver采用双循环架构：内部试错循环（inner trial loop）以当前状态为锚点，驱动自适应感知、在线规划和安全验证；外部演化循环（outer evolution loop）将完成的完整轨迹蒸馏为三种经验形式——可复用技能（skill）、策略（strategy）和安全经验（safety experience），并将其注入智能体的后续执行过程。整个框架无需模型训练，仅依赖经验累积与复用。

Result: 在机器人溶液配制（pH调节）任务中，完成时间减少48.2%，安全门拦截次数减少60.0%；在ALFWorld基准上，500项连续任务的20步内累计成功率从ReAct的76.2%提升至91.4%。两项实验均验证了框架在真实世界和模拟环境中的可行性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 学习式执行经验演化（learn-by-doing experience evolution）是实现闭环自动化科学发现的可行路径。LabEvolver证明，无需额外训练的轻量级经验累积机制能够在湿实验室及更广泛的任务环境中显著提升性能与安全性。

Abstract: We introduce LabEvolver, a training-free framework that equips safe and grounded wet-lab agents with episodic memory from execution experience. LabEvolver couples a state-grounded inner trial loop for adaptive perception, online planning, and safety validation with an outer evolution loop that distills completed trajectories into reusable skill, strategy, and safety experience. On robotic solution-preparation tasks, LabEvolver demonstrates real-world feasibility, reducing pH-regulation completion time and safety-gate intercepts by 48.2% and 60.0%, respectively. On ALFWorld, it further improves cumulative success rate within 20 steps from 76.2% with ReAct to 91.4% over 500 continual tasks, showing generality beyond wet-lab settings. These results support learn-by-doing experience evolution as a feasible path toward closed-loop automated scientific discovery. The project page is available at https://github.com/AndyGao6186/LabEvolver.

</details>


### [99] [Write-Safe Flow Field Mapping under Ambiguous Onboard Sensing and Localization Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27713)
*Linhao Jin,Qimin Feng,Peter Gunnarson,Qiang Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种地图参考感知的保守融合框架，通过预测局部速度场和学习写安全分数来持续衰减不确定的地图更新，从而解决移动机器人流场建图中因歧义观测和定位漂移导致的"幽灵结构"累积问题。在合成射流和横流环境中将幽灵污染降低42%，零样本硬件回放实验降低39%并保留81%地图覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人可从机载传感器推断局部流场结构，但局部合理估计并不总能安全写入全局地图。相似的流场结构可能产生歧义观测，而定位漂移会导致预测的补丁被写入错误位置，重复的错位更新会累积成持久的幽灵结构。现有方法缺乏对地图写入安全性的考量。

Method: 提出地图参考感知的保守融合框架。模型预测局部速度补丁和学习到的写安全分数，该分数连续衰减不确定的地图更新，同时在无可靠地图参考时允许初始化。采用软门控机制而非二值决策，实现连续的自适应融合。

Result: 在合成射流和横流环境中，该方法相比无门控融合平均减少42%的幽灵污染。零样本硬件回放实验使用真实压力和光流测量数据（推进器尾流），进一步将幽灵污染降低39%，同时保留81%的地图覆盖率。

Conclusion: 安全的图谱写入对歧义感知和定位漂移条件下的流场建图至关重要。地图参考感知的门控策略能有效抑制幽灵结构累积，同时保留足够的建图覆盖率，为移动机器人实时流场建图提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Mobile robots can infer local flow structure from onboard sensing, but a locally plausible estimate is not always safe to write into a global map. Similar flow structures may produce ambiguous observations, while localization drift causes predicted patches to be written at incorrect locations. Repeated misregistered updates then accumulate into persistent ghost structures. We address this failure mode with a map-reference-aware conservative fusion framework. The model predicts a local velocity patch and a learned write-safety score that continuously attenuates uncertain map updates while permitting initialization when no reliable map reference is available. Across synthetic jet and crossflow environments, the proposed method reduces average ghost contamination by 42% relative to ungated fusion. A zero-shot hardware replay using real pressure and optical-flow measurements from a thruster wake further reduces ghost contamination by 39% while retaining 81% map coverage. These results show that safe map writing is critical for flow mapping under ambiguous sensing and localization drift.

</details>


### [100] [RedFlow: Redirect Failure into Action-Level Corrections for Flow-matching VLA Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27782)
*Zhengyang Yan,Junhao Li,Fangqi Zhu,Zijun Wang,Quanxin Shou,Yikun Miao,Xiaoyi Pang,Zicong Hong,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RedFlow是一种细粒度的离线强化学习框架，通过将失败经验转化为动作级别的纠正性监督信号，用于流匹配视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的机器人操作任务。它包含上下文感知的纠正匹配和自适应重定向目标两个核心组件，在LIBERO基准和三个真实世界操作任务上优于SOTA离线RL基线，并将真实世界成功率从56.7%提升至74.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配VLA策略在部署中常因分布偏移产生累积误差。虽然离线RL可以利用展开数据改进策略，但现有方法要么忽略失败数据，要么仅在轨迹层面利用失败数据，导致学习效率低且错误持续存在。需要一种能细粒度利用失败经验的方法。

Method: RedFlow包含两个核心组件：(1) Context-Aware Corrective Matching——识别导致失败的动作，从相似上下文中检索成功的替代动作作为纠正目标；(2) Adaptive Redirection Objective——联合强化成功动作、抑制不良动作，并将可恢复的失败重定向至纠正目标。通过将成功和失败经验均转化为密集监督信号，实现从混合质量数据中进行鲁棒恢复学习。

Result: 在LIBERO基准和三个真实世界操作任务上，RedFlow一致优于SOTA离线RL基线，将真实世界成功率从56.7%提升至74.7%。同时匹配强在线方法（PPO、GRPO和DDPO）的性能，而训练样本量约少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过将失败经验细粒度地重定向为动作级别的纠正监督，RedFlow有效解决了VLA策略部署中的累积误差问题，实现了从混合质量数据中的高效恢复学习，在离线RL和在线RL方法之间取得了优越的样本效率与性能平衡。

Abstract: Flow-matching Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have shown strong potential for robotic manipulation but often suffer from compounding errors caused by distribution shifts during deployment. While offline reinforcement learning (RL) provides a practical way to improve deployed policies using rollout data, existing methods either ignore failure data or exploit it only at the trajectory level, resulting in low learning efficiency and persistent errors. We propose **RedFlow**, a fine-grained offline RL framework that redirects failure experiences into action-level corrective supervision for flow-matching VLA policies. RedFlow consists of two key components: (1) a **Context-Aware Corrective Matching** mechanism that identifies failure-inducing actions and retrieves successful alternatives from similar contexts as corrective targets, and (2) an **Adaptive Redirection Objective** that jointly reinforces successful actions, suppresses undesirable ones, and redirects recoverable failures toward corrective targets. By converting both successful and failed experiences into dense supervision, RedFlow enables robust recovery learning from mixed-quality data. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark and three real-world manipulation tasks show that RedFlow consistently outperforms state-of-the-art offline RL baselines, improving the real-world success rate from 56.7% to 74.7%. It also matches strong on-policy methods (PPO, GRPO, and DDPO) while requiring roughly an order of magnitude fewer training samples.

</details>


### [101] [DexDirect: Direct Kinesthetic Arm Guidance for Efficient Dexterous Demonstration Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27784)
*Beom Jun Kim,Shiu-Jen Wang,Jonathan Liu,Alvin Zhu,Quanyou Wang,Hanzhang Fang,Feng Xu,Mingzhang Zhu,Yuchen Cui,Dennis W. Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexDirect是一种直接动觉引导的灵巧操作演示收集界面：操作者通过手柄拖动六自由度重力补偿机械臂，同时用单个网络摄像头将操作者的另一只手重定向到16关节13自由度的灵巧机器人手。相比纯视觉(AnyTeleop)和姿态追踪(TeleDex)基线，收集成功率分别提高17.2倍和3.2倍，NASA-TLX显示显著降低了脑力需求、努力和挫败感，训练出的扩散策略在方块抓取放置任务上达到90%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作演示数据的高效收集是机器人学习的核心瓶颈。高保真界面成本高、设置复杂，而低成本替代方案控制精度差且增加操作者认知负担，亟需一种既低设置成本又高收集效率的演示采集方案。

Method: DexDirect采用直接动觉引导结合视觉手部重定向的两阶段方案：1）操作者通过手柄直接拖动6自由度重力补偿机械臂进行手臂运动控制，提供精确且直觉化的位姿输入；2）单摄像头捕捉操作者另一只手，通过重定向算法映射到16关节13自由度灵巧手，实现手部精细操作。

Result: 用户研究显示DexDirect比纯视觉基线(AnyTeleop)和姿态追踪基线(TeleDex)分别多收集17.2倍和3.2倍的成功演示；改进版NASA-TLX显示其在降低脑力需求、努力和挫败感方面表现优异，虽然物理需求有所增加；基于DexDirect数据训练的扩散策略在方块抓取放置任务上达到90%成功率。

Conclusion: 直接动觉手臂引导与基于视觉的手部重定向相结合，能够提供一种高效、低设置成本且可扩展的灵巧操作演示收集界面，有效缓解了演示数据收集的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Scalable collection of dexterous manipulation demonstrations remains a major bottleneck for robot learning. High-fidelity interfaces often require costly hardware and extensive setup, while low-setup, low cost alternatives tend to provide less precise control and impose greater cognitive workload on operators. We present DexDirect, a direct kinesthetic arm guidance for efficient dexterous demonstration collection. The operator drags a 6-DoF gravity-compensated robot arm directly by a handle, while a single webcam retargets operator's other hand onto a 16 joints 13-DoF dexterous robot hand. User studies suggest DexDirect collects 17.2x and 3.2x more successful demonstrations compared to purely vision (AnyTeleop) and pose-tracking (TeleDex) baselines. An adapted NASA-TLX shows DexDirect greatly reduces mental demand, effort, and frustration, despite raising physical demand. A diffusion policy trained on DexDirect demonstrations reaches a 90% success rate on a cube pick-and-place task. These results suggest that direct kinesthetic arm guidance combined with vision-based hand retargeting provides an efficient low-setup and scalable interface for collecting dexterous manipulation demonstrations

</details>


### [102] [RoboBRIDGE: A Modular Framework for Bridging Policies to Robust Real-World Robotic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27881)
*Sihyung Yoon,Minjong Yoo,Sanghyun Ahn,Seojeong Choi,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboBRIDGE是一个模块化框架，通过五个协调模块（Monitor、Perceptor、Planner、Controller、Robot Interface）将预训练的VLA模型系统性地转化为可靠的机器人智能体，解决故障恢复、长时程执行不一致及领域转移鲁棒性等问题，并在LIBERO、RoboCasa及真实场景中持续优于独立策略和先前的增强型VLA部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽然是有效的动作预测器，但作为机器人智能体部署时存在关键缺陷：缺乏故障恢复机制、长时程执行不一致、对观测/任务/形态变化的鲁棒性有限。已有解决方案通过模型重训练或环境特定模块分别处理这些问题，但缺乏一个通用的系统性框架来将预训练VLA转化为真正的机器人智能体。

Method: RoboBRIDGE采用模块化编排架构，包含五个协调模块：Monitor负责快速故障检测与分层恢复；Planner在环境偏离当前计划时触发重规划；Perceptor异步更新场景理解以避免执行阻塞；Controller通过原始技能微调将操作分解为域不变的基元，并使用专用LoRA适配器降低VLA使用时对领域转移的敏感性；Robot Interface负责与具体机器人平台的对接。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboCasa基准及跨多机器人平台和多种VLA主干网络的实际场景中，RoboBRIDGE一致性地优于独立的策略模型以及此前增强型的VLA部署方案。

Conclusion: 可靠的机器人智能体能力并非仅来自放大动作预测器本身，而是源于围绕这些预测器进行结构化编排。RoboBRIDGE通过系统化的模块协作，为从现成组件（包括预训练VLA）构建稳健的机器人智能体提供了一条通用路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have attracted growing interest as a scalable approach to robotic manipulation. While these models are effective action predictors, deploying them as robotic agents exposes critical gaps: no mechanism for failure recovery, inconsistent execution over long horizons, and limited robustness to shifts in observations, tasks, or embodiments. Existing solutions address these limitations individually through model retraining or environment-specific modules, yet what is needed is a general framework that systematically transforms a pretrained VLA into a robotic agent. We present RoboBRIDGE, a modular framework that provides an orchestration layer over five coordinated modules, namely Monitor, Perceptor, Planner, Controller, and Robot Interface, to compose robust robotic agents from off-the-shelf components, including pretrained VLAs. The Monitor pairs rapid failure detection with hierarchical recovery to correct errors before they cascade. When the environment diverges from the current plan, the Planner triggers replanning while the Perceptor updates scene understanding asynchronously, avoiding execution stalls. Within the Controller, primitive skill fine-tuning factors manipulation into domain-invariant primitives with dedicated LoRA adapters, reducing sensitivity to domain shifts when a VLA is used. Across LIBERO, RoboCasa, and real-world case studies spanning multiple robot platforms and VLA backbones, RoboBRIDGE consistently outperforms both standalone policies and prior augmented VLA deployments. These results suggest that reliable robotic agency does not arise from scaling action predictors alone, but from structured orchestration around them.

</details>


### [103] [Static In, Dynamic Out: Counterfactual Action Augmentation for Moving Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27890)
*Woo Chul Shin,Zhenyang Chen,Alfred Cueva,Nadun Ranawaka Arachchige,Yingyan Celine Lin,Benjamin Joffe,Shreyas Kousik,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SIDO是一种反事实动作增强方法，通过将静态物体演示策略适配到动态物体操作任务中，无需重新训练即可处理测试时的物体运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略在目标物体静止的操作任务中表现良好，但实际部署中物体经常运动（如传送带上的零件、风中摇曳的水果），现有策略无法处理这一假设违背问题。

Method: 将移动物体操作分解为两个子问题：预测物体未来位置，以及到达该预测位姿。通过将物体位移到反事实的未来位置，并变形演示动作块以保持手-物相对位姿，从而生成目标条件策略。部署时由物体位姿预测器提供未来位置。

Result: 在三个模拟任务（Mug, Square, Stack）的5种运动模式以及两个真实世界任务（Gantry, Peachtree）中，SIDO在移动物体操作上优于基线，同时保持静态物体性能不下降。

Conclusion: SIDO有效解耦了动态操作中的预测与到达两个子问题，使仅基于静态演示训练的策略能够泛化到未见过的物体运动场景，兼顾动态与静态操作性能。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies have advanced on manipulation tasks where the target object stays static during execution, but real deployments break this assumption: parts drift on conveyors and fruits sway in the wind. We introduce Static In, Dynamic Out (SIDO), a counterfactual action augmentation that enables a policy trained only on static object demonstrations to adapt to unseen object motion at test time. Our key idea is to factorize moving object manipulation into two sub-problems: predicting where the object will be, and reaching that predicted pose. SIDO displaces the object to a counterfactual future position and morphs the demonstrated action chunk to preserve the hand-object relative pose, yielding a goal-conditioned policy. At deployment an object pose predictor supplies the future position. Across three simulated tasks (Mug, Square, Stack) under five object motion patterns and two real-world tasks (Gantry, Peachtree), SIDO improves moving object success over the baselines while preserving static object performance. Project website: https://sido-staticindynamicout.github.io/.

</details>


### [104] [Learning Social Robot Navigation By Sensing Human Legs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27922)
*Alberto Vaglio,Andrea Garulli,Antonio Giannitrapani,Renato Quartullo,Tommaso Van Der Meer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CALF，一种端到端神经架构，直接从2D LiDAR扫描中解读腿部运动并生成安全的导航指令。配合自研的轻量级2D模拟器LegNav（包含新颖的行人步态模型），使用深度强化学习进行训练，并在TurtleBot 4上零样本部署验证，实现了平滑且社会合规的导航轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人导航中，2D LiDAR通常安装在接近地面的高度，感知到的主要是行人腿部而非整个人体，但现有学习方法仍将行人视为简单形状（如圆形），忽略了腿部运动的丰富信息。这一不匹配限制了导航的安全性和社会合规性。

Method: CALF结合卷积层、注意力机制和MLP构建端到端策略网络，直接从LiDAR扫描中提取腿部特征并输出导航指令；使用深度强化学习在自定义模拟器LegNav中训练，该模拟器将2D LiDAR射线追踪与新颖的行人步态模型相结合；用JAX实现，支持单GPU快速训练。

Result: CALF在导航性能和社会合规性上均优于经典方法与基于学习的方法的基线；通过TurtleBot 4零样本部署实验验证了其有效性，生成了平滑且社会合规的轨迹；训练时间在单张消费级GPU上不超过1小时。

Conclusion: 直接利用LiDAR中腿部运动信息的CALF方法比传统将行人视为简单形状的方法更有效，同时LegNav模拟器的高效性使得训练部署级策略成为可能，为行人感知导航提供了新思路。

Abstract: Robots navigating among pedestrians typically sense their surroundings with a 2D LiDAR mounted close to the ground. At that height, the sensor mostly sees moving legs rather than whole people, yet most learning-based navigation methods still treat pedestrians as simple shapes like circles. This paper addresses that gap with CALF (Convolutional Attention for Leg Features), an end-to-end neural architecture that combines convolutional layers, attention, and MLP to interpret leg motion directly from LiDAR scans and produce safe navigation commands. The CALF policy is trained using deep reinforcement learning algorithms within LegNav, a custom lightweight 2D simulator that combines 2D LiDAR ray tracing with a novel pedestrian gait model. The resulting policy is compared against classical and learning-based baselines in terms of navigation performance and social compliance. The approach is validated through real-world experiments via zero-shot deployment on a TurtleBot 4, yielding smooth and socially compliant trajectories. Written in JAX, the LegNav simulator enables the training of a deployment-ready CALF policy in under an hour on a single consumer GPU.

</details>


### [105] [RaDiVe: Robust 4D Radar Odometry with Distance-Bounded NDT and Velocity-Discrepancy Point Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28045)
*Sangwoo Jung,Dongjae Lee,Chiyun Noh,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RaDiVe是一个面向4D雷达里程计的框架，通过距离受限的NDT配准、基于速度不一致性的点不确定性建模，以及基于SDF隐式神经映射的表面点提取，在多个公开数据集上将平移ATE平均降低44.4%、旋转ATE平均降低21.3%，同时保持实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D雷达虽能在恶劣天气下提供稳健感知，但雷达点云固有的稀疏性、噪声和有限的位置精度给基于配准的里程计带来了显著挑战，现有方法难以兼顾精度、鲁棒性和实时性。

Method: 提出三种核心方法：1）距离受限的Normal Distributions Transform（NDT），通过限制对应搜索到近距离体素对来提升优化稳定性和计算效率；2）速度不一致性点不确定性模型，根据实测多普勒径向速度与从估计自车速度预测的径向速度之间的差异对每个点进行加权；3）基于SDF隐式神经映射的表面点提取，构建几何一致且噪声滤除的局部子地图。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上，RaDiVe相比现有4D雷达里程计基线，平移ATE平均降低44.4%，旋转ATE平均降低21.3%，且保持实时性能。

Conclusion: RaDiVe通过改进NDT配准、利用多普勒速度信息进行不确定性建模，以及借助隐式神经映射构建高质量子地图，显著提升了4D雷达里程计的精度和鲁棒性，代码将开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in 4D radar enable robust perception in adverse weather; however, the inherent sparsity, noise, and limited positional precision of radar point clouds pose significant challenges for registration-based odometry. In this letter, we propose RaDiVe, a 4D radar odometry framework designed to improve the accuracy and robustness of radar point-cloud registration. We introduce a distance-bounded Normal Distributions Transform (NDT), which improves optimization stability and computational efficiency by restricting the correspondence search to near-distance voxel pairs. To mitigate measurement ambiguity, we propose a velocity-discrepancy point uncertainty model that weights each input 4D radar point according to the discrepancy between its measured Doppler radial velocity and the radial velocity predicted from the estimated ego-velocity. Furthermore, we incorporate Signed Distance Function (SDF)-based surface point extraction via implicit neural mapping to construct a geometrically consistent and noise-filtered local submap. Evaluations across multiple public datasets demonstrate that RaDiVe outperforms existing 4D radar odometry baselines by 44.4% in translational Absolute Trajectory Error (ATE) and 21.3% in rotational ATE on average, while maintaining real-time performance. The source code will be made publicly available to the robotics community: https://github.com/to-be-open-sourced.

</details>


### [106] [UniCross: Unified Cross-Skill Dexterous Manipulation Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28198)
*Hui Zhang,Julian Ferchow,Jie Song,Mirko Meboldt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，将灵巧操作中的四种典型技能（抓取、重定位、手中旋转、手中平移）建模为单一公式，共享相同的状态空间、动作空间和共同的目标结构，从而支持跨技能策略的蒸馏、泛化、鲁棒性以及长时程操作的平滑组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数方法将灵巧操作的四种核心技能（抓取、重定位、手中旋转、手中平移）分别建模，采用各自特定的动作约束、目标函数甚至专用的手部形态，破坏了技能之间的兼容性和连续性，难以实现长时程操作中的流畅组合。

Method: 构建一个统一的公式框架，将四种技能统一在同一状态空间和动作空间下，并共享相同的目标结构；基于该公式蒸馏出单一跨技能策略，该策略在所有技能上表现优异，且能在不同手部形态间迁移。

Result: 单一跨技能策略在每种技能上均表现良好，能够泛化到未见过的物体，对干扰保持鲁棒，并能无缝衔接多种技能以完成长时程操作；框架还能在不同手部形态间有效迁移。

Conclusion: 不同灵巧操作技能本质上可视为同一共享任务公式的实例化，揭示了不同行为之间的内在一致性，为长时程灵巧操作提供了统一的建模视角。

Abstract: Many dexterous manipulation tasks require the object to remain securely held throughout the interaction. From the perspective of hand-object relational motion, such manipulation comprises four canonical skills: grasping, relocation, in-hand rotation, and in-hand translation. Human hands flexibly compose these skills to accomplish complex tasks. Existing approaches, however, model these skills separately with skill-specific action constraints, objectives, or even dedicated hand morphologies, which breaks the compatibility and continuity required for long-horizon composition. In this work, we present a unified framework that models all four skills in a single formulation that shares the same state and action spaces and a common objective structure. This formulation enables straightforward distillation of a single cross-skill policy that performs strongly on every skill, generalizes to unseen objects, stays robust to disturbances, and chains skills seamlessly into long-horizon manipulation. The framework also transfers effectively across different hand morphologies. Overall, our results suggest that different dexterous manipulation skills can be viewed as instantiations of a shared task formulation, revealing the intrinsic consistency across different behaviors.

</details>


### [107] [When Robots Exchange Meaning: A Demo of Goal-Oriented Semantic Communications for Collaborative Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28256)
*Peizheng Li,Xinyi Lin,Sajida Gufran,Adnan Aijaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一个机器人-边缘语义通信（SemCom）测试平台，将机器人端视觉压缩、边缘端语义建图与仪表盘交互相结合，为面向任务导向的6G语义通信研究提供了实践平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作机器人是6G中典型的任务导向用例，其通信质量应体现在任务执行、环境理解和闭环操作上，而非仅关注数据包传输。需要一种将通信与任务语义深度融合的方法及其实验平台。

Method: 搭建了机器人-边缘语义通信测试平台：移动机器人配备RGB-D相机和LiDAR运行ROS 2；边缘端使用Jetson Orin进行重建、RTAB-Map语义建图、语义物体处理及浏览器可视化。机器人端通过ONNX Runtime编码器将RGB帧压缩为VQ-VAE token，边缘端用PyTorch解码器重建，并与深度、位姿和3D建图信息关联生成语义地图。

Result: 320×240的图像被表示为80×60的token网格，打包有效载荷为5400字节，相对于模型输入RGB字节实现了42.67倍的压缩比。重建的视觉流被关联深度与位姿信息生成语义地图，验证了从语义视觉传输到物体级地图交互的完整路径。

Conclusion: 该演示平台展示了从语义视觉传输到物体级地图交互的完整流程，为未来6G网络中语义通信、具身AI和物理AI赋能的机器人交叉研究提供了实用平台。

Abstract: Collaborative robotics is a representative task-oriented 6G use-case, where communication quality should be reflected in mission execution, environment understanding, and closed-loop operation rather than packet delivery alone. This demo paper presents a robot-edge semantic communication (SemCom) testbed integrating robot-side visual compression, edge-side semantic mapping, and dashboard-based mission interaction. A mobile robot equipped with RGB-D sensing and LiDAR runs ROS 2, while a Jetson Orin edge node performs reconstruction, RTAB-Map mapping, semantic object handling, and browserbased visualization. As an initial proof of concept, RGB frames are encoded on the robot into VQ-VAE tokens using an ONNX Runtime encoder and reconstructed on the edge using a PyTorch decoder. A 320 X 240 image is represented by an 80 X 60 token grid with a packed payload of 5400 bytes, corresponding to a 42.67X reduction relative to model-input RGB bytes. The reconstructed visual stream is further associated with depth, pose, and 3D mapping information to generate a semantic map for downstream robotic applications. The demo exposes the full path from semantic visual transport to object-level map interaction, and provides a practical platform for future task-aware 6G networking studies at the intersection of SemCom, embodied AI, and physical AI-enabled robotics. A video of the demo is available at https://tinyurl.com/Tos09

</details>


### [108] [SemAnCorr: Semantic Anchored Correspondence for Zero-Shot Manipulation Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28382)
*Xiaoxiang Dong,William Baron,Hongyi Chen,Uksang Yoo,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SemAnCorr是一种无需训练的框架，通过联合位姿-对应关系优化选择语义一致的锚点区域，并利用功能映射在物体表面传播对应关系，从而实现跨几何异构物体的稠密对应和操作技能迁移，在PartNet-Mobility基准上达到90.8%的语义准确率，并且仅需单次演示即可实现零样本操作技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨物体实例（功能相似但几何不同）的操作技能迁移是机器人学习中的根本挑战。现有对应方法依赖最近邻特征匹配，常产生空间不连贯的对应关系，无法恢复局部几何坐标系，导致技能迁移不可靠。

Method: SemAnCorr采用训练免费（training-free）框架，通过联合位姿-对应关系优化（joint pose-correspondence optimization）选取语义一致的锚点区域，再利用功能映射（functional maps）将这些约束在物体表面进行传播，从而同时保证对应关系的语义一致性和几何连贯性。

Result: 在基于PartNet-Mobility构建的稠密对应基准上达到90.8%的语义准确率，相比近期最优基线显著提升几何连贯性；在实际机器人操作中，仅需单次演示即可实现比现有方法更可靠的零样本操作技能迁移。

Conclusion: SemAnCorr验证了通过联合优化和功能映射传播能够同时兼顾语义一致性与几何连贯性，从而有效解决跨几何异构物体的技能迁移难题，并展示了其在实际机器人操作中的直接应用价值。

Abstract: Transferring manipulation skills across object instances that share functionality but differ in geometry remains a fundamental challenge in robot learning. While recent correspondence methods leverage dense visual descriptors and 3D feature fields, nearest-neighbor feature matching often produces spatially incoherent correspondences that fail to recover the local geometric frames required for reliable skill transfer. We introduce SemAnCorr, a training-free framework that establishes dense correspondence by selecting semantically consistent anchor regions through joint pose-correspondence optimization and propagating these constraints over the object surface using functional maps. The resulting correspondences preserve both semantic consistency and geometric coherence, enabling object-centric manipulation skills to transfer across geometrically diverse instances. We evaluate SemAnCorr on a dense correspondence benchmark built on PartNet-Mobility, achieving 90.8% semantic accuracy in our benchmark evaluation while improving geometric coherence over recent state-of-the-art baselines. Finally, we show that these improvements translate directly into real-world manipulation performance: using a single demonstration, SemAnCorr enables substantially more reliable zero-shot manipulation skill transfer to previously unseen objects than existing correspondence methods. Videos and additional visualizations are available at [https://semancorr.github.io](https://semancorr.github.io) .

</details>


### [109] [TacWAM: Anchor-Guided World Action Model with Mechanics-Aware Tactile Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28391)
*Lei Jin,Yiding Ma,Xin Zhang,Chen Gao,Wei Wu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacWAM是一个力学感知的触觉世界动作模型，通过空间对齐融合触觉编码器、触觉历史编码器和锚点引导三模态注意力三个步骤，将未来触觉预测与机器人动作生成相结合，在四个真实接触丰富的操作任务中达到75.0%的平均成功率，比最强基线高出37.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）主要依赖视觉未来预测，虽然能捕捉场景结构和物体运动，但对接触丰富操作中的力、形变、剪切和滑动等物理信息提供有限的监督。因此需要触觉未来预测来提供有意义的物理信息，同时避免触觉成为动作生成的捷径特权线索。

Method: TacWAM通过三步解决该问题：(1) 空间对齐融合（SAF）触觉编码器将触觉外观、密集力场和形变流映射到共享的潜在预测空间，并通过双边力和力矩重建保留全局接触信息；(2) 触觉历史编码器提供时间上下文，使未来触觉预测反映力和形变的动态变化；(3) 锚点引导三模态（AGT）注意力将当前视觉/触觉锚点、未来预测token和动作token分离，使未来触觉状态仅用于监督训练而不被动作分支直接读取。

Result: TacWAM在四个真实世界接触丰富操作任务（脆弱抓取、持续表面接触、动态手内操作）中达到75.0%的平均成功率，超过最强基线37.5个百分点。分阶段消融实验显示，移除触觉历史或放宽对未来预测目标的访问时性能持续下降。

Conclusion: 未来触觉监督结合信息丰富的触觉表征和部署一致性信息约束时，能够有效提升接触感知的动作学习，验证了力学感知触觉未来预测对接触丰富操作的改进价值。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) combine future-state prediction with robot action generation, but existing approaches largely rely on visual futures. Visual prediction captures scene structure and object motion, yet provides limited supervision for force, deformation, shear, and slip during contact-rich manipulation. This creates two design requirements: tactile futures should carry meaningful physical information, and they should not become privileged cues for action generation. We present TacWAM, a mechanics-aware tactile WAM that addresses this challenge in three steps. First, a Spatially Aligned Fusion (SAF) Tactile Encoder maps tactile appearance, dense force fields, and deformation flow into a shared latent prediction space, with bilateral force and torque reconstruction preserving global contact information. Second, a tactile history encoder provides temporal context so future tactile prediction reflects how force and deformation change beyond the current tactile observation. Third, Anchor-Guided Tri-Modal (AGT) Attention separates current visual and tactile anchors, future prediction tokens, and action tokens, allowing future tactile states to supervise training without being directly read by the action branch. We evaluate TacWAM on four real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks covering fragile grasping, sustained surface contact, and dynamic in-hand manipulation. TacWAM achieves an average success rate of 75.0%, exceeding the strongest evaluated baseline by 37.5 percentage points. Staged ablations show consistent degradation when tactile history is removed and access to future prediction targets is relaxed. These results indicate that future tactile supervision can improve contact-aware action learning when combined with informative tactile representations and deployment-consistent information constraints.

</details>


### [110] [FasTac: A Curved Multispectral Vision-Based Tactile Sensor for High-Speed High-Precision 3D Shape and Force Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28416)
*Xiaofan Lu,Kaiji Huang,Jiahui Chen,Yuankai Lin,Hua Yang,Zhouping Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FasTac，一种曲面视觉触觉传感器，集成多光谱光度立体视觉、动态卷积力估计和FPGA硬件加速，实现了高精度3D重建、三轴力估计和高速处理的统一，深度MAE降至0.0415 mm，力估计NMAE约2.4%，处理延迟仅1.09 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有曲面视觉触觉传感器难以在紧凑外形中同时实现精确的3D重建、三轴力估计和高吞吐处理。为了灵巧操作的触觉感知需求（分辨细粒度接触几何、区分法向/切向载荷、捕捉瞬态信号），需要克服这一多目标权衡。

Method: 1) 基于单图像传感器同时多光谱成像，获得空间对齐观测用于鲁棒表面法向估计；2) 利用边界先验的快速泊松深度重建；3) 提出HyperForce，使用位置感知动态卷积分区建模曲面弹性体的非均匀力学响应并估计三轴力；4) 将完整图像-法向-力流水线部署于FPGA。

Result: 近红外照明与边界先验将深度MAE从0.2730 mm降至0.0415 mm；HyperForce对法向力和剪切力的NMAE分别为2.74%和2.39%；FPGA部署将延迟从GPU的3.26 ms缩短至1.09 ms。多物体重建、反馈抓取和振动测量验证了细粒度几何感知、稳定力反馈和动态接触感知能力。

Conclusion: FasTac通过多光谱光度立体视觉、动态卷积力估计和FPGA硬件加速的有机融合，首次在同一紧凑曲面传感器中兼顾了高精度几何重建、三轴力估计和高速实时处理，为实现灵巧操作提供了可行的传感方案。

Abstract: Curved tactile fingertips for dexterous manipulation must resolve fine contact geometry, distinguish normal and tangential loads, and capture transient signals. Existing curved vision-based tactile sensors struggle to combine accurate 3D reconstruction, three-axis force estimation, and high-speed processing in a compact form. This article presents FasTac, a curved vision-based tactile sensor integrating multispectral photometric stereo, dynamic-convolution force estimation, and hardware acceleration on a field-programmable gate array (FPGA). Single-image-sensor simultaneous multispectral imaging provides spatially aligned observations for robust surface normal estimation, followed by boundary-prior fast Poisson depth reconstruction. HyperForce uses position-aware dynamic convolution to model the spatially nonuniform mechanical response of curved elastomers and estimate three-axis forces. The complete image-to-normal-force pipeline is deployed on an FPGA. Experiments show that near-infrared (NIR) illumination and the boundary prior decrease depth mean absolute error (MAE) from 0.2730 mm to 0.0415 mm; HyperForce achieves normalized mean absolute error (NMAE) values of 2.74% and 2.39% for normal and shear forces, respectively; and FPGA deployment shortens processing latency from 3.26 ms on the GPU to 1.09 ms. Multi-object reconstruction, feedback grasping, and vibration measurement validate fine geometric perception, stable force feedback, and dynamic contact sensing.

</details>


### [111] [One Future, Every Robot: Label-Efficient Collective-State Prediction with Decentralized JEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28443)
*Alan-Barsag Gazzaev,Alexey Garvilov,Sergey Muravyov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出集体状态JEPA（CS-JEPA），一种用于群机器人分布式共享状态预测的递归联合嵌入预测架构。每台机器人仅利用16帧局部历史和每条有向边64个浮点数的递归消息，即可预测共同的未来token场，无需全局池化、目标编码器或未来动作记录。在预训练后通过岭探针评估，CS-JEPA在预测误差和机器人间一致性标签预算AUC上全面优于同等部署容量的基线，并在动作条件分支价值预测中降低45.5%的MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群机器人系统面临的核心挑战是：在只有局部观测和带宽受限消息的条件下，每台机器人能否预测相同的未来集体状态。这一"分布式共享状态预测"问题尚未得到系统解决，而预测未来集体状态对于协调、规划与决策至关重要。

Method: 提出Collective-State JEPA（CS-JEPA），一种递归联合嵌入预测架构。核心设计包括：(1) 每台机器人从16帧局部历史提取表征；(2) 通过每条有向边64浮点数的递归消息进行机器人间通信；(3) 输出为所有机器人共享的"未来token场"；(4) 不使用全局池化、目标编码器、回合时钟或录制的未来动作。预训练无需下游集体标签，评估时用岭探针在6/12/24个全局标记片段上拟合。

Result: 在五种子前瞻性注册实验中，CS-JEPA在预测误差和机器人间一致性标签预算AUC上优于具有9,607个额外训练参数但同等推理容量的基线，覆盖环状、互kNN和未见过规模（最多108台机器人）等多种拓扑族，5/5外部种子全部有利。在独立密封八种子实验中，与动作条件预测器相比，CS-JEPA将分支价值MSE降低45.5%，候选分数Pearson相关系数提升0.1291，8/8种子均有利，包括未见过N=32的情况。

Conclusion: 共同未来JEPA目标可作为分布拓扑和规模变化下群机器人预测的标签高效原始原语，并额外展现出规划相关的价值估计能力，为分布式群智能体的集体预测与决策提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Can every robot in a swarm predict the same future collective state from only local observations and bandwidth-limited messages? We formulate this as decentralized shared-state prediction and introduce Collective-State JEPA (CS-JEPA), a recurrent joint-embedding predictive architecture whose output at every robot represents one common future token field. At deployment, each robot uses a 16-frame local history and one 64-float recurrent message per directed edge; there is no global pooling, target encoder, episode clock, or recorded future action. After pretraining without downstream collective labels, frozen representations are evaluated with ridge probes fitted on 6, 12, or 24 globally labeled episodes. Against raw-future reconstruction with the same receiver anchor and deployment capacity but 9,607 additional training-only parameters, a prospectively registered five-seed follow-up improves prediction-error and inter-robot-agreement label-budget AUC on in-distribution, ring, mutual-kNN, and unseen-size families up to 108 robots. Every effect favors CS-JEPA in 5/5 outer seeds. In a separate sealed eight-seed follow-up, matched action-conditioned predictors receive each candidate four-step plan before producing receiver-local predictive representations. CS-JEPA reduces branch-value MSE by 45.5% and improves within-context candidate-score Pearson correlation by 0.1291, with both effects favorable in 8/8 seeds, including at unseen N=32. These results support common-future JEPA targets as a label-efficient primitive for decentralized swarm prediction under topology and size shift, with additional evidence of planning-relevant value estimation.

</details>


### [112] [Machines that know they are aging: a framework for hardware-aware autonomous intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28451)
*Cheng Siong Chin,Jianhua Zhang,Mohan Venkateshkumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Aging-Aware Autonomous Intelligence (AAAI)框架，将硬件健康状况直接整合到自主系统的推理、规划和任务执行中，通过三大支柱（硬件自感知、自适应推理、生存导向智能）实现闭环认知架构，解决自主系统因硬件老化导致的"能力失配"和"无源性崩溃"问题，提高系统韧性和延长使用寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统的AI通常假设硬件保持初始状态，但实际上电池退化、传感器漂移、处理器时序错误累积、内存可靠性下降等不可避免的老化过程会造成预期能力与实际能力的失配，进而导致"无源性崩溃"——任务失败源于硬件累积退化而非单一组件故障。这种能力失配问题在无法维保或安全性至关重要的环境中尤为严峻。

Method: AAAI框架建立在三大支柱之上：(1) 硬件自感知——利用物理失效模型持续估计电源、传感、存储和计算子系统的健康状态；(2) 自适应推理——根据剩余硬件能力调整推理复杂度、规划时域和任务优先级；(3) 生存导向智能——通过性能优化、资源节约和优雅降级，在任务目标间分配剩余运行寿命。该框架不引入新硬件，而是将预测性维护、生命周期管理和硬件感知计算统一为闭环认知架构。

Result: 论文提出了一种统一的理论框架，将硬件健康感知纳入自主系统的认知闭环。通过使机器能够识别并响应自身的衰老过程，AAAI有望改善系统韧性、延长运行寿命，并支持更安全、更优雅的任务完成，尤其在太空任务、海洋机器人和植入式医疗设备等不可接触或安全关键型环境中。

Conclusion: 硬件老化感知与自主智能的集成是自主系统在不可接触或安全关键环境中运行的必备条件。AAAI通过闭环认知架构实现硬件健康与推理规划的深度融合，为提升系统弹性和延长运行寿命提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous systems inevitably age, yet their artificial intelligence typically assumes hardware remains in its original condition. Batteries degrade, sensors drift, processors accumulate timing errors, and memory reliability declines, creating a growing mismatch between assumed and actual capability. This can lead to agnostic collapse, where mission failure arises from accumulated hardware degradation rather than a single component fault. We propose Aging-Aware Autonomous Intelligence (AAAI), a framework that integrates hardware health directly into reasoning, planning, and mission execution. AAAI is built on three pillars: hardware self-awareness, which continuously estimates the health of power, sensing, memory, and computation subsystems using physics-of-failure models; self-adaptive reasoning, which adjusts inference complexity, planning horizon, and task priorities according to remaining hardware capability; and survival-centric intelligence, which allocates remaining operational life across mission objectives through performance optimization, resource conservation, and graceful degradation. Rather than introducing new hardware, AAAI unifies prognostics, lifecycle management, and hardware-aware computing into a closed-loop cognitive architecture. We argue that such integration is essential for autonomous systems operating in inaccessible or safety-critical environments, including space missions, marine robotics, and implantable medical devices. By enabling machines to recognize and respond to their own aging, AAAI improves resilience, extends operational lifetime, and supports safer, more graceful mission completion.

</details>


### [113] [TEA-AgriVLN: Traversability Estimation Alarm for Agricultural Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28474)
*Xiaobei Zhao,Xingqi Lyu,Xin Chen,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TEA模块（Traversability Estimation and Alarm），通过估计图像可穿越性并在此与预测动作不一致时提醒决策者重新思考，解决了农业场景中可穿越性模糊的问题，将TEA集成到AgriVLN骨干网络中构建TEA-AgriVLN方法，在A2A基准上SR从0.47提升至0.54，NE从2.91m降至2.70m，达到农业VLN-CE领域的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN-CE方法从室内扩展到农业场景时面临一个关键差异：室内场景中区域是否可穿越易于分类（如木地板可穿越、混凝土墙不可穿越），而农业场景中可穿越性往往是模糊的（如未成熟的玉米地对机器狗可穿越但对人可能不可穿越），因此需要专门解决农业场景中可穿越性判断的不确定性问题。

Method: 提出TEA模块，包含两个核心步骤：（1）估计相机图像的可穿越性，得到可穿越性地图；（2）当预测的低层动作与可穿越性地图不一致时，向决策者发出警报促使其重新思考。该模块被集成到AgriVLN骨干网络中，形成TEA-AgriVLN方法。

Result: 在A2A基准上，TEA-AgriVLN将Success Rate从0.47提升到0.54，Navigation Error从2.91m降低到2.70m，取得农业VLN-CE领域的最佳性能（SOTA）。同时进行了消融实验和案例研究，分析了TEA在不同地面类别和场景类别上的有效性与局限性。

Conclusion: 通过可穿越性估计与动作一致性告警机制，TEA-AgriVLN有效解决了农业场景中可穿越性模糊的问题，验证了可穿越性感知在农业VLN-CE任务中的价值，并公开了代码以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires an agent to follow a natural language instruction, predicting a sequence of low-level actions to navigate a robot from a starting point to a target location. The A2A benchmark and the AgriVLN method pioneeringly extended VLN-CE from indoor scenes to agricultural scenes, while we observed a challenging distinction: In indoor scenes, whether a zone is traversable tends to be clear to classify, such as wood floors are traversable but concrete walls are not. In agricultural scenes, however, this issue tends to be ambiguous, such as an unripe cornfield might be traversable for a robotic dog but might be non-traversable for a human. To address this issue, we propose the TEA module, which estimates the traversability of the camera image, then alarm the decision-maker for rethinking when the predicted action does not align with the traversability map. We integrate it into the AgriVLN backbone to build our TEA-AgriVLN method. When evaluated on A2A, it improves Success Rate (SR) from 0.47 to 0.54 and Navigation Error (NE) from 2.91 m to 2.70 m, showing the state-of-the-art performance in the agricultural VLN-CE domain. We further implement the ablation studies and the case study, discussing the effectiveness and limitations of TEA on different ground categories and scene classes. Code: https://github.com/AlexTraveling/TEA-AgriVLN.

</details>


### [114] [X-NavDP: Generalizing Navigation Diffusion Policy to Novel Behavior and Embodiments with Group Q-score Reweighted Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28560)
*Tianyu Yang,Yiming Zeng,Wenzhe Cai,Yuqiang Yang,Jiaqi Peng,Hui Cheng,Jiangmiao Pang,Tai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GQRM框架，一种数据高效的扩散策略RL后训练方法，通过自举探索和分组Q分数归一化，将预训练导航扩散策略微调为支持跨异构机器人的X-NavDP，在仿真中将成功率从61.20%提升到84.28%，在真实世界困难场景中从10%提升到65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练导航扩散策略依赖大规模专家演示，但这些数据由针对单一标称机器人的全知oracle规划器生成，限制了策略对多种具身形态和挑战场景（如死胡同、长障碍绕行）的泛化能力。RL后训练是原则性解决方案，但先前RL for diffusion方法改进有限，原因是扩散策略的不可解似然导致策略梯度不稳定且策略探索效率低下。

Method: 提出GQRM（Group Q-score Reweighted Matching）框架，包含两个互补设计：(i) 自举探索策略与行为扰动相结合，保留预训练策略先验；(ii) 分组Q分数归一化机制，在每个状态上计算逐轨迹价值，实现高效的重加权分数匹配。通过在异构具身形态上进行分布式在线RL训练来微调策略。

Result: 微调后的策略X-NavDP在跨具身视觉导航任务上达到最先进水平，仿真场景整体成功率从61.20%提升到84.28%，真实世界困难场景成功率从10%提升到65%。

Conclusion: GQRM框架通过数据高效的扩散RL后训练，显著增强了预训练导航扩散策略的跨具身泛化能力和困难场景应对能力，展示了RL后训练在扩散策略中的有效性。

Abstract: Pretraining navigation diffusion policies rely on large-scale expert demonstrations. These data are typically generated by a fully-informed oracle planner suited to a single nominal robot. This limits the policy's generalization to diverse embodiments and challenging scenarios (e.g., escaping dead ends or detouring long obstacles) that demand diverse local reactive behaviors with only onboard local observations. Post-training the policy with reinforcement learning (RL) offers a principled remedy. However, previous RL for diffusion approaches lead to only marginal improvements. This is because the intractable likelihood of diffusion policies renders policy gradients unstable in addition to inefficient policy exploration. To address these challenges, we propose a data-efficient diffusion RL post-training framework - GQRM (Group Q-score Reweighted Matching). Our framework introduces two complementary designs: (i) a self-bootstrapped exploration strategy with behavior perturbation that preserves the pretrained policy prior, and (ii) a group Q-score normalization mechanism that computes per-trajectory values on each state for efficient reweighted score matching. By conducting distributed online RL training across heterogeneous embodiments, the resulting fine-tuned policy, X-NavDP, achieves state-of-the-art cross-embodiment visual navigation performance, improving the overall success rate from 61.20% to 84.28% in simulation and 10% to 65% in real-world hard cases. The code and model are publicly available at https://yty-sky.github.io/x-navdp-project-page.

</details>


### [115] [FA-RDP: A Frequency-Adaptive Reactive Diffusion Policy for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28596)
*Lifeng Zhuo,Wendi Chen,Han Xue,Shirun Tang,Jun Lv,Cewu Lu,Chuan Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FA-RDP是一种频率自适应的反应式扩散策略，通过共享多频视觉-力Transformer在不同频率下预测动作块，并利用学习到的多模态指示器动态切换低频多步采样（接触前）与高频单步采样（接触后），从而解决扩散策略在接触丰富操作中多模态保持与力反馈反应性之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接触丰富的操作任务中，动作多模态性和反应性在单集的不同阶段占据主导：接触前需保留多种可行的轨迹模式，而接触后需快速响应力反馈。但标准扩散策略在整集中使用固定的推理频率和采样步数，导致根本性权衡：低频多步采样能保留接触前多模态但反应慢，高频采样反应快但易导致接触前模式坍缩。

Method: FA-RDP包含两个核心组件：(1) 共享多频视觉-力Transformer，同时以低频和高频预测动作块；(2) 学习到的多模态指示器，根据动作歧义程度动态选择接触前的多步低频采样或接触后的单步高频采样。此外引入Manifold Consistency Distillation (MCD)，将扩散网络重参数化以在机器人动作流形上预测动作，同时保留基于DDPM的残差监督。

Result: 在三个接触丰富的操作任务上，FA-RDP取得最高成功率，同时保留多样化的接触前轨迹模式。

Conclusion: FA-RDP通过自适应切换推理频率有效解决了接触丰富操作中多模态保持与反应性之间的固有矛盾，验证了频率自适应扩散策略在该类任务中的有效性。

Abstract: In contact-rich manipulation, action multimodality and reactivity dominate different stages of a single episode. Before contact, multiple trajectories might be equally valid, making it important to preserve diverse action modes. After contact, geometric constraints and force limits narrow the solution space, while successful execution demands rapid responses to force feedback. However, standard diffusion policies use a fixed inference frequency and sampling steps throughout the episode, forcing a fundamental compromise: low-frequency, multi-step sampling better preserves pre-contact multimodality but responds slowly to force feedback, whereas high-frequency sampling improves reactivity but tends to collapse distinct pre-contact modes. To resolve this tradeoff, we present FA-RDP, a frequency-adaptive reactive diffusion policy. A shared multi-frequency visual-force Transformer predicts action chunks at both low and high frequencies, while a learned multimodality indicator dynamically selects multi-step low-frequency sampling before contact and one-step high-frequency sampling as action ambiguity decreases. We further introduce Manifold Consistency Distillation (MCD), which reparameterizes the diffusion network to predict actions on the robot action manifold while retaining DDPM-based residual supervision. Experiments on three contact-rich manipulation tasks show that FA-RDP achieves the highest success rate while preserving diverse pre-contact trajectory modes. Code and videos are available at https://fa-rdp.github.io.

</details>


### [116] [PAC-MAN: Perception-Aware CBF-RL for Whole-Body Safety in Humanoid Dodgeball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28623)
*Lizhi Yang,Junheng Li,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PAC-MAN框架，将控制屏障安全（CBF）与部署现实感知（头戴相机的分割遮罩深度）耦合，用于全人形机器人躲避球任务。在离线的any-link接触基准上接近特权状态预言机性能；发现CBF实用性与感知可观测性相关，最终在Unitree G1真机上零样本部署，95%成功率，并能用语义分割躲避不同球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身躲避动态威胁（如dodgeball）需要安全性与部署现实感知兼顾。现有CBF方法通常假设特权状态信息（准确的球状态），但真实部署中机器人只能获得有限感知（固定头戴相机），且需容忍不完美感知与观测遮挡。作者旨在探索感知与安全屏障结构之间的耦合关系，并实现可在真实硬件上零样本部署的安全躲避策略。

Method: 提出PAC-MAN框架：(1) 部署策略仅依赖头戴相机提供的分割遮罩深度图作为观测；(2) 训练阶段使用CBF引导，其清障约束覆盖每个身体连杆（Link-CBF/Joint-CBF）；(3) 引入对抗性运动先验正则化，塑造获得的躲避反射行为；(4) 在可控any-link接触基准上，以固定投掷的两类场景（单投掷与部署循环，含走动返回站点并恢复）进行离线评估，并对比特权状态预言机、固定相机、云台跟踪和特权运行时滤波等感知设置。

Result: 离线基准中，固定头戴相机的策略性能仅比特权状态预言机低几分，说明单独的固定相机足以完成躲避。屏障结构的可用性依赖于感知可观测性：Joint-CBF在准确球状态下达最优，但在固定相机训练引导下性能退化，而配合云台跟踪或特权运行时滤波可恢复。真机实验中，轻量Link-CBF策略在Unitree G1上零样本部署，容忍不完美感知，对95%投掷成功躲避，并利用语义分割躲避不同球。

Conclusion: PAC-MAN证明感知-屏障耦合设计对全身躲避策略至关重要：安全屏障在训练中的作用受感知可观测性制约，而轻量Link-CBF策略在真实机器人上展现鲁棒性与泛化能力（不同球、不完美感知）。研究为具身安全控制在感知受限条件下的部署提供了实践依据。

Abstract: We present PAC-MAN, a perception-aware CBF-RL framework that couples control-barrier safety with deployment-realistic onboard sensing for whole-body humanoid dodgeball. The deployed policy sees the ball only as segmentation-masked depth from a head-mounted camera, while training-time CBF guidance represents clearance to every body link, and an adversarial motion prior regularizes the resulting evasive reflexes. We evaluate on a controlled any-link contact benchmark with seeded throws in two regimes: single throws and a deployment loop in which the robot walks back to its station and recovers between throws. On this benchmark, the policy comes within a few points of a privileged state oracle: a fixed onboard camera alone is adequate for evasion. We find that usable barrier structure depends on perceptual observability: Joint-CBF gives the best performance with accurate ball states, degrades under fixed-camera observations when used only as training guidance, and recovers with a ball-tracking gimbal or privileged runtime filter. We therefore deploy a lightweight Link-CBF policy zero-shot on the Unitree G1 in the real world, where it tolerates imperfect perception, succeeds on 95% of throws, and uses semantic segmentation to dodge different balls.

</details>

### [117] [Breaking Scale Separation in Metamaterials' homogenization: Interface-Inertia-Enhanced Relaxed Micromorphic Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27385)
*Angela Madeo,Leonardo Perez,Mohammad Sarhil*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了机械超材料均质化描述中的一个全新概念——宏观界面的惯性贡献。通过扩展弛豫微形态模型，在边界引入动能表面能，使均质化模型能够区分具有相同体性质但仅界面构型不同的有限尺寸试样，从而捕捉微观结构在边界截断所产生的界面惯性效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统均质化连续介质模型依赖分离尺度的经典假设（波长远大于单胞尺寸），但在有限尺寸超材料样品和更高频率下该假设逐渐失效，动态响应强烈受微观结构在外部边界的截断方式影响。现有均质化模型无法捕捉这一边界依赖的动态行为差异。

Method: 作者扩展了弛豫微形态（relaxed micromorphic）模型，在考虑物体的边界上引入动能表面能，产生了边界条件中的额外惯性项，作为微观结构截断产生的界面惯性的均质化对应物。该方法保持了弛豫微形态模型的变分结构，同时使连续体能够再现全分辨率模拟中观察到的边界依赖响应。

Result: 研究表明，同一晶格的不同截断方式会产生具有不同质量分布的边界，导致有限尺寸试样的动态响应出现可测量的差异。所提出的均质化模型现在能够区分体性质相同但仅界面构型不同的有限尺寸试样，特别在高频区域能够再现全分辨率模拟中的边界依赖响应。

Conclusion: 该工作为机械超材料的均质化描述引入了宏观界面惯性的新概念，通过表面动能能的引入，突破了传统均质化模型无法区分不同边界截断构型的局限，为高频区域有限尺寸超材料试样的动态行为预测提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Homogenized continuum models are widely used to describe wave propagation and band-gap behavior in mechanical metamaterials without explicitly resolving their microstructure. Their validity, however, typically relies on the classical separation of scales assumption, according to which the wavelength of the propagating disturbance is much larger than the characteristic size of the unit cell. In finite-size metamaterial samples and at higher frequencies, this assumption progressively breaks down, and the dynamic response becomes strongly influenced by the way the microstructure is truncated at the external boundaries. In this work we introduce a fundamentally new concept in the homogenized description of mechanical metamaterials: the inertial contribution of macroscopic interfaces. We show that different truncations of the same lattice generate boundaries with distinct mass distributions, which lead to measurable differences in the dynamic response of finite-sized specimens. To capture this complex mechanism in a homogenized framework, we extend the relaxed micromorphic model by introducing a kinetic surface energy defined on the boundary of the considered body. This generates an additional inertial term in the boundary conditions that can be seen as the homogenized counterpart of the interface inertia produced by the truncation of the microstructure. As a result, the homogenized model can now distinguish between finite-sized specimens that share identical bulk properties but differ only in the configuration of their interfaces. The proposed formulation preserves the variational structure of the relaxed micromorphic model while enabling the continuum to reproduce boundary-dependent responses observed in fully resolved simulations, particularly in frequency regimes ....... See the PDF for the full abstract.

</details>


### [118] [Exact Total Variation Minimizers as Non-Oscillatory Limiters in High-Order Methods for Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27416)
*Gabriel P. Langlois,Jerome Darbon,Rongjie Lai,Chi-Wang Shu,Xiangxiong Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出通过全变差（TV）去噪后处理步骤抑制守恒律高精度数值方法在间断附近产生的伪振荡。针对一般分析算子下TV最小化不满足次模性质的问题，采用微分包含算法精确求解TV最小化器，将其作为限制器应用于傅里叶伪谱方法和五阶有限差分格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度数值方法（如伪谱法和高阶有限差分格式）在守恒律间断解附近会产生非物理的伪振荡（Gibbs现象），需要有效的后处理限制器来抑制这些振荡，同时保持数值精度和物理性质。

Method: 提出对数值解施加TV去噪后处理步骤，通过最小化节点值的总变差来抑制振荡。由于一般分析算子下该离散最小化问题不满足现有精确最大流算法所需的次模性质，改用微分包含算法精确求解TV最小化器。一维算法有限步收敛、无需参数调整且保持总质量守恒；二维情形逐维应用一维算法。

Result: 将所提方法作为后处理限制器，应用于傅里叶伪谱方法和五阶有限差分格式，测试了标量守恒律、可压缩欧拉方程和二维不可压欧拉方程，验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TV去噪作为后处理限制器能有效抑制高精度方法在间断附近的伪振荡，微分包含算法为不满足次模性质的TV最小化问题提供了精确求解途径，且算法具有有限步收敛、无参数调节和质量守恒等良好性质。

Abstract: High-order numerical methods for conservation laws can generate spurious oscillations near discontinuities. We propose to suppress these oscillations by post-processing the numerical solution with a total variation (TV) denoising step that reduces the total variation of the nodal values. For a general analysis operator, the resulting discrete minimization problem need not satisfy the submodularity property that existing exact max-flow algorithms require, so we instead compute the TV minimizer exactly using a differential inclusion algorithm. In one dimension, the differential inclusion algorithm computes the TV minimizer in finitely many steps, requires no tuning of algorithmic parameters, and preserves the total mass. In two dimensions, we apply the 1D algorithm dimension by dimension. We test the method as a post-processing limiter for Fourier pseudospectral methods and a fifth-order finite difference scheme applied to scalar conservation laws, compressible Euler equations, and the two-dimensional incompressible Euler equations.

</details>


### [119] [Dynamical Low-Rank Filters for Data Assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27432)
*Yoshihito Kazashi,Youssef Marzouk,Fabio Nobile,Fabio Zoccolan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于随机微分方程（SDEs）数据同化问题的动力学低秩（DLR）类型滤波器，包括联合最小化均值和协方差误差的DLRA滤波器以及高效纳入相关正交方向的策略，并扩展到Kalman-Bucy型滤波器和集合方法，还提出了一种在非线性场景中展现潜力的粒子型DLRA滤波器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对基于SDEs的数据同化问题，现有滤波器在处理高维非线性系统时面临计算成本高、数值不稳定等挑战，需要开发更高效且能适应非高斯分布和复杂漂移项的滤波方法。

Method: 首先推导了一种可联合最小化均值和协方差误差的DLRA滤波器，并提出了高效纳入相关正交方向的策略，使主子空间能根据观测算子演化。这些方法自然扩展到线性漂移情况下的Kalman-Bucy型滤波器，以及适用于非线性漂移和非高斯分布的集合方法。此外还提出了一种初步的粒子型DLRA滤波器。

Result: 数值模拟表明这些方法在相关应用中展现有效性和可行性，DLRA滤波器在均值和协方差误差的联合最小化方面表现良好，正交方向纳入策略提升了主子空间的演化能力，粒子型DLRA滤波器在非线性设置中展现出潜力。

Conclusion: 所提出的DLR类型滤波器族为高维SDE数据同化问题提供了高效且灵活的解决方案，值得进一步深入研究这些滤波方向。

Abstract: We propose dynamical low-rank (DLR) type filters for data-assimilation problems based on stochastic differential equations (SDEs). In detail, first we derive a DLRA filter for minimizing jointly the mean and covariance error, as well as a strategy to efficiently include the relevant orthogonal directions. This last approach allows the main subspace to evolve also according to the observation operator. Those procedures naturally extend to a Kalman-Bucy type filter when dealing with linear drift, and to ensemble methods, too, resulting also suitable for problems described by nonlinear drift and possible non-Gaussian distribution. Moreover, we further propose a preliminary particle-type DLRA filter that shows potentiality in nonlinear settings. Numerical simulations show the efficacy of these procedures in relevant applications, opening up to further studies in these filtering directions.

</details>


### [120] [Comparison of a Parametric Physics-Informed Neural Network and a Tensorial Reduced-Order Model for the Shallow-Water Dam-Break Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27433)
*Anton Myshak,Md Rezwan Bin Mizan,Ilya Timofeyev*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文开发了两种参数化数据驱动降阶模型——物理信息神经网络（PINN）和非侵入式张量降阶模型（TROM），用于参数化一维浅水溃坝问题，两者均无需时间积分，直接学习从空间、时间和溃坝参数到物理状态的映射。研究表明引入激波感知配置点对提升PINN模型的鲁棒性至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对参数化一维浅水溃坝问题，传统数值模拟计算成本高昂，需要开发无需时间积分的低成本数据驱动降阶模型，以快速预测不同参数下的物理状态。

Method: 提出两种降阶模型：1）PINN（物理信息神经网络），利用物理方程约束训练网络；2）非侵入式张量降阶模型（TROM），采用张量分解学习直接解映射。两种模型均直接建立从空间、时间和溃坝参数到物理状态的映射关系，避免时间步进积分。PINN模型中引入激波感知配置点以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 对样本外参数和外推参数进行了详细比较，结果表明两种降阶模型均能有效预测；同时验证了激波感知配置点在提高PINN模型鲁棒性方面的关键作用。

Conclusion: PINN和TROM两种数据驱动降阶模型均可作为参数化浅水溃坝问题的高效求解方案，但激波感知配置点是PINN模型获得可靠结果的重要前提。

Abstract: We develop two parametric data-driven reduced models: a physics-informed neural network (PINN) and a non-intrusive tensorial reduced-order model (TROM), and apply both approaches to the parametrized one-dimensional shallow-water dam-break problem. Both reduced models do not require time integration and learn a direct solution map from space, time, and dam-break parameters to the physical state. We present a detailed comparison for out-of-sample and extrapolated parameter values. In addition, we demonstrate that it is essential to introduce shock-aware collocation to improve the robustness of the PINN model.

</details>


### [121] [A Unified Discrete Gradient-SAV Framework for Structure-Preserving Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27795)
*Elena Celledoni,David Martín de Diego,Brynjulf Owren,Miguel Vaquero*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将离散梯度（DG）方法与标量辅助变量（SAV）方法相结合的框架，用于为耗散系统和保守系统构建保结构积分器。通过SAV二次化将动力学提升到扩展状态空间，使修正能量满足精确的离散梯度恒等式，从而得到三种不同精度和成本配置的积分器：一阶半隐式前向欧拉格式、二阶自伴随中点格式和二阶隐式性降低的预测格式。该构造可推广到几乎泊松系统，并在可执行的离散条件下保持选定的Casimir不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耗散系统和保守系统的数值积分需要保结构特性，但传统离散梯度方法在处理复杂能量函数时往往面临隐式程度高、计算成本大的问题。SCV方法虽然能简化能量处理，但尚未与离散梯度方法系统结合，缺乏一种兼顾结构保持性和计算效率的通用框架。

Method: 利用SAV二次化将原动力学系统提升到扩展状态空间，在该空间中修正能量满足精确的离散梯度恒等式，从而允许使用离散梯度方法构造保结构积分器。基于此框架推导出三种积分器：一阶Forward Euler格式、二阶自伴随Midpoint格式和二阶Predictive格式（降低隐式性）。并将框架推广到几乎泊松系统，通过可执行的离散条件保持Casimir不变量。

Result: 数值实验涵盖Allen-Cahn方程、Ohta-Kawasaki型非局部梯度流、双阱Hamiltonian振荡器以及具有非线性三次Casimir的泊松系统，验证了所提出的三种积分器在不同系统上的结构保持性能和数值精度。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将离散梯度方法与SAV方法有机融合，提供了一套从一阶到二阶、隐式性可调节的保结构积分器族，并具备对几乎泊松系统的推广能力和Casimir不变量保持能力，为耗散和保守系统的数值模拟提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We present a framework combining discrete gradient (DG) methods with the Scalar Auxiliary Variable (SAV) approach to construct structure-preserving integrators for dissipative and conservative systems. The key observation is that SAV quadratization lifts the dynamics to an extended state space on which the modified energy has an exact discrete-gradient identity. This viewpoint yields three integrators with different accuracy and cost profiles: a first-order semi-implicit Forward Euler scheme, a second-order self-adjoint Midpoint scheme, and a second-order Predictive scheme with reduced implicitness. The construction extends to almost-Poisson systems and preserves selected Casimir invariants under an enforceable discrete condition. Numerical experiments cover the Allen--Cahn equation, an Ohta--Kawasaki-type nonlocal gradient flow, a double-well Hamiltonian oscillator, and a Poisson system with a nonlinear cubic Casimir.

</details>


### [122] [Dynamical Low-Rank Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27438)
*Youssef Marzouk,Fabio Nobile,Fabio Zoccolan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动态低秩逼近（DLRA）的平滑方法，用于高维数据同化问题。作者将先前开发的联合均值-协方差优化（JMCO）滤波框架扩展到Rauch-Tung-Striebel递归，推导出降阶平滑器，并为仿射漂移动力学建立了对应的Kalman-Bucy平滑。该算法在保持DLRA自适应性的同时，显著降低了平滑过程的计算时间和存储需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据同化中的平滑过程通常因计算成本过高而难以实施，亟需一种既能降低计算负担又能保持精度的降阶方法。

Method: 基于动态低秩逼近（DLRA）框架，将联合均值-协方差优化（JMCO）滤波设置扩展至平滑场景，通过Rauch-Tung-Striebel递归实现降阶平滑器，并对仿射漂移动力学推导相应的Kalman-Bucy平滑公式。

Result: 所提出的算法在保留DLRA自适应特性的前提下，显著减少了整个平滑过程的计算时间和存储需求。

Conclusion: DLRA方法成功扩展至高维数据同化的平滑问题，为计算密集型平滑任务提供了一种高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Computational costs often make smoothing procedures prohibitive for high-dimensional data assimilation problems. To address this challenge, we propose a dynamical low-rank approximation (DLRA) methodology for smoothing concerning frameworks based on stochastic differential equations. We extend the previously developed joint mean-and-covariance optimization (JMCO) filtering setting to derive a reduced-order smoother via the Rauch--Tung--Striebel recursion and establish the corresponding Kalman--Bucy smoothing for affine drift dynamics. The resulting algorithms retain the adaptive nature of DLRA while significantly reducing the computational time and storage of the whole smoothing procedure.

</details>


### [123] [Residual-Driven Lifting Identification for Nonlinear-Manifold Reduced-Order Models of Parametrized Linear PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27471)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非线性流形降阶模型的训练通常依赖高保真快照数据来拟合非线性提升算子，这在实践中可能成本高昂或难以获取。作者希望在不依赖快照的情况下，仅利用问题本身的残差信息来训练非线性降阶模型，从而降低训练成本并保持精度。

Method: 在给定潜在空间和提升空间的条件下，将逼近表示为低维潜在坐标加非线性提升映射。不拟合快照数据，而是通过最小化基于残差计算的状态误差上界来确定提升算子。对于仿射参数化算子，目标函数支持离线-在线分解，提升更新可转化为一系列低维加权最小二乘问题。

Result: 在对流-扩散问题和平面应变弹性基准上的数值评估表明，该方法相比线性子空间显著提高了精度，且达到与快照驱动的非线性流形训练相当的精度，同时避免在提升识别阶段使用高保真快照。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了利用残差驱动训练非线性流形降阶模型的可行性，在避免高保真快照依赖的同时获得了与快照驱动方法相当的精度，为参数化PDE降阶建模提供了新的高效训练范式。

Abstract: We introduce a residual-driven procedure for training nonlinear-manifold reduced-order models for parametrized linear partial differential equations that, given prescribed latent and lifting spaces, identifies the nonlinear lifting without high-fidelity solution snapshots. The approximation is represented by a low-dimensional latent coordinate together with a nonlinear lifting into a richer reduced space. Rather than fitting the lifting to snapshot data, we determine it by minimizing a computable residual-based upper bound for the state error. For affinely parametrized operators, the resulting training objective admits an efficient offline--online decomposition, and the lifting update reduces to a sequence of low-dimensional weighted least-squares problems. Numerical evaluations on an advection--diffusion problem and a plane-strain elasticity benchmark show that the proposed approach substantially improves accuracy over linear subspaces. The resulting nonlinear models achieve accuracy comparable to snapshot-driven nonlinear-manifold training while avoiding high-fidelity snapshots in the lifting-identification stage.

</details>


### [124] [Scalable Graph Coreset Selection via Greedy Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27602)
*Zhaiming Shen,Alexander Cloninger*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最小内积贪心选择规则的列选择性图采样算法，无需特征分解或全局图遍历，仅访问拉普拉斯矩阵的小随机列子集，适用于无法在内存中存储完整拉普拉斯的大规模图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从大规模图中采样代表性节点是图信号处理和网络分析的基础任务，但现有方法需要访问完整的图拉普拉斯矩阵，在大规模图场景下（无法存储全矩阵于内存）不切实际。

Method: 提出一种列选择性图采样算法，基于最小内积贪心选择规则。每次迭代仅访问拉普拉斯矩阵的少量随机列子集，无需特征分解或全局图遍历。在随机块模型下进行分析，考虑节点间度分布均衡的情形。

Result: 当度分布均衡时，算法实现与簇大小成比例的采样；对于Paley-Wiener空间中的带限图信号，均值估计得到控制，且误差随簇间连通性减弱而衰减。合成和真实数据的数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该算法在无需存儲完整拉普拉斯矩阵的前提下，实现大规模图上的高效代表性采样，理论与实验均证明其有效性。

Abstract: Sampling representative nodes from large graphs is fundamental to graph signal processing and network analysis, yet existing methods require access to the full graph Laplacian, making them impractical at scale. We propose a simple and effective column-selective graph sampling algorithm based on a minimum inner product greedy selection rule. At each iteration, the algorithm accesses only a small random subset of Laplacian columns, requiring no eigendecomposition or global graph traversal, making it well-suited for large-scale graphs where the full Laplacian cannot be stored in memory. We analyze the algorithm under the stochastic block model and show that, when the degree distribution is balanced across nodes, the algorithm achieves sampling proportional to cluster size, and that the resulting mean estimate is controlled for band-limited graph signals in the Paley-Wiener space, with the error decaying as inter-cluster connectivity weakens. Numerical experiments on both synthetic and real-world data validate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [125] [A novel sampling method for reconstruction of a moving point acoustic source in $\mathbb{R}^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27621)
*Guanqiu Ma,Shuang Li,Hongxia Guo*
