<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动点源的定位与轨迹恢复是逆源问题中的基础性难题，现有方法通常需要较多观测点且计算效率不高。本文旨在减少观测点数量、提高计算效率与噪声鲁棒性。

Method: 结合代数理论与几何知识，利用五个观测点数据证明源位置唯一性；构建基于到达时间差约束残量在真实源位置处为零的指示函数，通过采样方法重构移动源的空间位置与发射时刻。

Result: 算法将观测点数量减少至五个，同时提升了计算效率、稳定性和抗噪声能力；数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该采样方法以较少的观测数据实现了移动点源的轨迹恢复，在减少观测点需求的同时保持了较高的计算效率与噪声抵抗能力。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a novel sampling method to recovering the trajectory of a moving point source in R^3, where both the spatial location and emission moment of the moving point source are unknown. Combining algebraic theory with geometric knowledge, we prove the uniqueness of the source location by using measured data from five observation points. Our sampling method constructs an indicator function based on the property that the residual of the time difference of arrival constraint formula vanishes at the true source location. It achieves the reconstruction of the spatial positions and emission moments of a moving point source only using data from five observation points and their corresponding arrival times. The algorithm not only reduces the required number of observation points, but also improves computational efficiency, stability, and noise resistance. Numerical experiments verify the effectiveness of the method.

</details>


### [126] [A spectral-compensated scheme for space-parameter Poisson noise functionals: error bounds and complexity estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27657)
*Yun-Ching Chang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在由Lévy测度λ_β(u)du构成的Gel'fand三元组中计算泊松空间噪声泛函P'(u)，通过引入三个离散化参数（小振幅截断、Donsker delta截断M和混沌阶数N），并利用匹配高斯空间噪声补偿被丢弃的小振幅，将Wasserstein-1误差从O(ε^{1-α/2})提升至O(ε)，同时将计算复杂度从O(τ^{-2α/(2-α)})降至O(τ^{-α})。数值实验通过Gil-Pelaez反演确认了所有理论预测的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松空间噪声在随机偏微分方程和测度值过程中具有广泛应用，但其数值离散化面临小振幅截断带来的系统性偏差。传统方法丢弃小振幅跳跃导致误差随截断衰减缓慢，且计算复杂度高。作者旨在通过匹配高斯噪声补偿来改进误差收敛速率并降低计算负担。

Method: 在Gel'fand三元组框架下研究由稳定型Lévy测度λ_β(u)=cu^{-1-α}(0<α<2)生成的泊松空间噪声泛函。引入三个离散化参数：(1)小振幅截断，将丢弃的小振幅跳跃用匹配的Gaussian空间噪声替代；(2)Donsker delta截断M；(3)混沌展开阶数N。理论分析Wasserstein-1误差、残差渐近正态性及计算复杂度。数值验证采用Gil-Pelaez反演进行确定性数值实验。

Result: 高斯补偿将Wasserstein-1误差从O(ε^{1-α/2})改进至O(ε)，残差在O(ε^{α/2})阶上渐近正态。计算复杂度从O(τ^{-2α/(2-α)})降至O(τ^{-α})。M截断以代数速率收敛，N截断以超几何速率收敛。对Gamma型边界(α=0)和指数缓变情形也进行了分析，所有预测速率均与确定性数值实验高度吻合。

Conclusion: 匹配高斯空间噪声补偿策略能显著提升泊松空间噪声离散化的精度与效率，研究结果为稳定型Lévy噪声驱动的模型提供了理论保证和实用的数值方案。

Abstract: This paper computes Poisson space noise functionals, $P^{\prime}(u)$, realised in a Gel'fand triple built from a Lévy measure $λ_β(u)du$. We isolate three discretisation parameters: a small-amplitude cut-off, a Donsker delta truncation M, and a chaos order N. For a stable-type intensity $λ_β(u)=cu^{-1-α}$ ($0<α<2$), replacing discarded small amplitudes with matched Gaussian space noise improves the Wasserstein-1 error from $O(ε^{1-α/2})$ to $O(ε)$. The residual is asymptotically normal at $O(ε^{α/2})$. This compensation reduces computational complexity from $O(τ^{-2α/(2-α)})$ to $O(τ^{-α})$. We also evaluate the Gamma-type boundary ($α=0$) and exponential tempering. Truncations converge algebraically (M) and super-geometrically (N). All predicted rates are tightly confirmed by deterministic numerical experiments via Gil-Pelaez inversion, eliminating Monte Carlo noise.

</details>


### [127] [Numerical Spectrum Linking: Identification of Governing PDE via Koopman-Chebyshev Approximation with Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27728)
*Phonepaserth Sisaykeo,Shogo Muramatsu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个数值框架，通过在统一的Chebyshev谱域中建立观测驱动与方程驱动的Koopman算子之间的联系，从观测数据中识别控制偏微分方程（PDE）。该框架利用Chebyshev谱表示构造有限维Koopman算子，并引入统一的观测模型与最小二乘系数恢复公式，支持任意采样网格上的PDE识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法（如动态模态分解DMD）近似Koopman算子时未显式将其与微分算子关联，难以直接用于PDE的识别与比较。因此需要一种能够在观测数据与候选控制PDE之间建立直接数值对应关系的框架，以便从观测中可靠地识别出控制方程。

Method: 在公共Chebyshev谱域中，分别构造观测驱动的Koopman算子（从数据中恢复）和方程驱动的Koopman算子（由候选PDE推导），使二者可直接比较。引入统一观测模型，通过最小二乘系数恢复公式从任意采样网格上的观测数据中恢复Chebyshev谱系数，从而实现观测与基于Chebyshev的Koopman分析之间的数值一致接口。

Result: 在直接Chebyshev采样、均匀采样和不规则采样三种配置下的数值实验表明，该框架能准确从观测数据中识别控制PDE。观测密度研究表明，当足够数量的独立观测可用以保证系数恢复稳定时，能够稳定实现可靠的PDE识别，为实际应用提供了指导性准则。

Conclusion: 该框架为从观测数据中识别PDE提供了一条有效且实用的数值路径，通过Chebyshev谱域统一了数据驱动与方程驱动的Koopman分析，并在不同采样场景下展示出良好的鲁棒性与稳定性。

Abstract: A numerical framework is proposed for identifying governing partial differential equations (PDEs) from observational data by establishing a link between observation-driven and equation-driven Koopman operators in a common Chebyshev spectral domain. In contrast to data-driven approaches such as dynamic mode decomposition (DMD), which approximate Koopman operators without explicitly relating them to differential operators, the proposed framework constructs finite-dimensional Koopman operators using Chebyshev spectral representations, thereby enabling direct comparison between data-derived dynamics and candidate governing PDEs. A unified observation model together with a least-squares coefficient recovery formulation is introduced to recover Chebyshev spectral coefficients from observations obtained on arbitrary sampling grids. This provides a numerically consistent interface between practical observations and Chebyshev-based Koopman analysis. Numerical experiments under direct Chebyshev, uniform, and irregular sampling configurations demonstrate that the proposed framework accurately identifies the governing PDE from observations. An observation-density study shows that reliable PDE identification is consistently achieved once sufficient independent observations are available for stable coefficient recovery, providing a practical guideline.

</details>


### [128] [An Asymptotic-Preserving Dynamical Low-Rank Semi-Lagrangian Method for Multiscale Linear Kinetic Transport Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27736)
*Shun Li,Yan Jiang,Mengping Zhang,Tao Xiong*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种渐近保持（AP）的动态低秩半拉格朗日方法，用于求解多尺度线性动力学输运方程。该方法结合了半拉格朗日离散的大时间步长能力和低秩表示的计算效率，并利用采样角求积策略降低通量导数计算的复杂度，在扩散极限下保持AP性质，数值实验验证了其稳定性与高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度线性动力学输运方程在动力学与扩散 regimes 之间的数值求解具有挑战性，传统方法要么受限于小时间步长，要么在高维度下计算和存储成本过高。需要发展一种既能保证大时间步长稳定，又能降低高维计算复杂度的方法。

Method: 将渐近保持（AP）的半拉格朗日离散与动态低秩表示相结合，提出一种混合方案：使用宏观密度更新近似与基于基更新的Galerkin积分器共同处理动力学分布函数。通量导数通过采样角求积策略计算以保持低秩复杂度，并对常系数情形下全求积低秩方案进行了无条件稳定性分析。

Result: 对常系数情形建立了全求积低秩方案的无条件稳定性分析，量化了角采样在通量导数计算中引入的误差，证明了格式在扩散极限下的AP性质。高维数值实验表明，该方法具备AP性质、在大时间步长下稳定，并能在动力学与扩散区间内保持计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的AP动态低秩半拉格朗日方法在多尺度动力学输运方程求解中兼顾了大时间步长稳定性与低秩计算效率，是一种在高维情况下具有实用前景的数值方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop an asymptotic-preserving (AP) dynamical low-rank semi-Lagrangian method for multiscale linear kinetic transport equations. The method combines the large-time-step capability of semi-Lagrangian discretizations with the storage and cost reduction provided by low-rank representations. The proposed scheme couples an approximate macroscopic density update with the basis update Galerkin integrator for the kinetic distribution. To retain the reduced complexity in the semi-Lagrangian flux evaluation, the flux derivative is computed through a sampled angular quadrature strategy. We establish an unconditional stability analysis of the full-quadrature low-rank scheme in the constant-coefficient case. The error induced by angular sampling in the flux derivative is quantified. The resulting scheme is shown to be AP in the diffusive limit. Numerical experiments, including high-dimensional test cases, demonstrate that the proposed method is AP, stable under large time steps, and computationally efficient across kinetic and diffusive regimes.

</details>


### [129] [An Adaptive Finite Element Method for Marker-Driven Level-Set Transport on Hierarchical Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28104)
*Eugenio Aulisa,Samuele Baldini,Giacomo Barbi,Andrea Chierici,Sandro Manservisi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于水平集输运的自适应有限元框架，通过对零水平集附近窄带区域进行动态局部细化，在显著降低计算成本的同时达到与均匀离散化相当的精度，适用于二维和三维的结构化与非结构化网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水平集方法在两相流界面捕获中广泛应用，但传统均匀网格离散化在需要高精度捕捉界面时计算成本高昂，亟需一种能够动态分配计算资源、在界面附近集中分辨率而其他地方保持粗网格的自适应框架，以在保证精度的同时降低计算开销。

Method: 该方法基于树形分层网格（tree-based hierarchical mesh）实现动态局部细化与粗化，仅在零水平集周围窄带区域集中细化。水平集场通过自适应细化网格上的标记物输运（marker transport）进行更新，实现界面预测并构建随界面演化的自适应层级；同时采用后向特征追踪精确计算对流后的水平集场。两项操作均使用高效的多层标记物与点定位算法来确定细化层级间的包含单元关系。

Result: 在二维和三维的结构化与非结构化网格（四边形、单纯形、楔形及六面体网格）上进行数值实验，经历严重界面变形条件下，自适应策略达到了与均匀离散化相当的精度，但计算成本大幅降低；同时在强自适应条件下保持了良好的守恒特性。

Conclusion: 该自适应框架为水平集多相流求解器提供了高效且灵活的实现方案，可自然集成到现有求解器中，为大规模界面输运问题提供更经济可行的数值求解途径。

Abstract: This work presents a new adaptive finite-element framework for level-set transport, achieving high accuracy in kinematic interface transport problems relevant to interface-capturing methods for two-phase flows while reducing computational cost. This framework accommodates dynamic refinement and coarsening, and is compatible with standard finite-element data structures. The algorithm is applicable to both structured and unstructured discretizations in two and three dimensions. The method uses a tree-based hierarchical mesh with dynamic local refinement that tracks the evolving interface, concentrating resolution in a narrow band around the zero level set while preserving a coarse discretization elsewhere. The level-set field is updated through marker transport on adaptively refined meshes, enabling interface prediction and the construction of an evolving adaptive hierarchy. Concurrently, backward characteristic tracing provides accurate evaluation of the advected level-set field. Both operations employ an efficient multilevel marker and point-location algorithm to identify containing elements across refinement levels. Numerical experiments on two- and three-dimensional structured and unstructured grids, including quadrilateral, simplicial, wedge, and hexahedral meshes, subjected to severe interface deformations, demonstrate that the adaptive strategy achieves accuracy comparable to uniform discretizations at a substantially lower cost, while maintaining good conservation properties under aggressive adaptivity. Consequently, the proposed approach provides an efficient and flexible framework that can be naturally integrated into level-set-based multiphase flow solvers.

</details>


### [130] [R3MG-C: a high-order algebraic-geometric multilevel preconditioner for continuous finite element discretizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28235)
*Davide Polverino,Marco Feder,Luca Heltai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种代数-几何多重网格预条件器，通过R树算法递归划分有限元支撑点、嵌入局部不连续多项式粗空间，为高阶连续拉格朗日有限元离散提供稳健高效的求解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典代数多重网格（AMG）对低阶椭圆偏微分方程离散十分有效，但在高阶有限元方法中性能显著退化，需要一种无需网格层级或区域分解、仅依赖有限元支撑点坐标的自动构造预条件器。

Method: 利用R树算法基于轴对齐包围盒递归划分支撑点，构造凝聚层级；在凝聚盒上通过连续节点插值嵌入度为p'>0的局部不连续多项式空间，作为Nicolaides粗空间的高阶推广；通过Galerkin方式构建层级和延拓算子。

Result: 二维和三维数值实验表明，作为共轭梯度预条件器的V-cycle迭代次数保持稳定，且在标准AMG失效的区域内依然有效。两层分析量化了粗多项式次数如何抵消大凝聚块和高阶细离散的影响。

Conclusion: 该代数-几何多重网格方法为高阶有限元离散提供了一种自动、稳健、高效的预条件方案，克服了经典AMG在高阶场景下的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Algebraic multigrid (AMG) methods are robust and efficient black-box preconditioners for linear systems arising from low-order discretizations of elliptic partial differential equations, but their performance often deteriorates for high-order methods. We introduce an algebraic-geometric multilevel preconditioner for continuous lagrangian finite element discretizations. The method automatically constructs prolongation operators and a Galerkin hierarchy using only the coordinates of finite element support points, without requiring a prescribed mesh hierarchy or domain decomposition. The support-point are recursively partitioned by an R-tree algorithm based on axis-aligned bounding boxes, producing a hierarchy of agglomerates. In contrast to classical AMG, which typically uses piecewise-constant aggregate coarse spaces, our method embeds local discontinuous polynomial spaces of degree $p'>0$, defined on the agglomerate boxes, through continuous nodal interpolation. This yields a high-order extension of the Nicolaides coarse space. A two-level analysis quantifies how the coarse polynomial degree offsets the effects of large agglomerates and high-order fine discretizations, under uniform box-regularity, interpolation-stability, and stable-decomposition assumptions. Numerical experiments in two and three dimensions show that the resulting V-cycle, used as a conjugate-gradient preconditioner, maintains stable iteration counts and remains effective in regimes where standard AMG deteriorates.

</details>


### [131] [A MATLAB Tool for the Stable Generation of Matrix Polynomial Evaluation Schemes with Two-Product Savings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28286)
*J. Ibáñez,J. Sastre,J. M. Alonso,E. Defez*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种软件驱动的方法，将矩阵多项式求值的2个矩阵乘积（$2M$）节省从孤立的特例推广到任意次数的多项式（$m \in \{18,21,24,26,27,28\}$及所有$m \ge 30$），通过MATLAB实现利用可变精度算术求解非线性方程组、验证系数稳定性并计算精度边界，数值实验表明在保持或提升经典Paterson--Stockmeyer方法精度的同时系统性地实现了$2M$的理论减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有理论发展证明了消除两个矩阵乘积（$2M$）的求值方案是可行的，但文献中仅记录了孤立情况下的稳定实例（如特定次数的矩阵指数和对数的Taylor逼近），缺乏适用于任意多项式的通用方法。为填补这一空白，需要开发一种通用的、可验证稳定性的软件驱动流程，将计算节省推广到一般多项式。

Method: 本文提出一个软件驱动的自动化流程：(1)针对次数$m \in \{18,21,24,26,27,28\}$及所有$m \ge 30$的多项式，构造消除$2M$的求值方案；(2)通过求解非线性方程组（SNEs）确定底层求值系数；(3)引入自动化验证例程，筛选并验证数值稳定的系数集，确保浮点执行下的鲁棒性；(4)利用MATLAB中的可变精度算术（VPA）处理SNEs、验证稳定性并预测精度边界。该方法仅要求多项式非零首项系数的一般性假设。

Result: 数值实验涵盖多种矩阵函数，结果表明所开发的实现能够保持甚至在某些情况下提升经典PS方法的数值精度，同时系统性地实现了$2M$的理论计算减少。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了$2M$减少策略在任意多项式求值中缺乏通用方法的空白，提供了一种可复现的软件工具，使矩阵函数计算效率的理论优势得以在实践中稳定实现，且不影响数值精度。

Abstract: Computing numerical approximations of matrix functions frequently relies on the efficient evaluation of high-degree matrix polynomials. Although computational bounds are historically governed by the Paterson--Stockmeyer (PS) method, recent theoretical developments have demonstrated the viability of evaluation schemes that eliminate two matrix products ($2M$). Existing literature documents stable instances of this $2M$ reduction only for isolated cases, such as specific degrees of Taylor approximations for the matrix exponential and the matrix logarithm. However, a generalized approach for arbitrary polynomials remains unestablished. To address this limitation, this work presents a software-driven procedure that extends these computational savings to polynomials of degrees $m \in \{18, 21, 24, 26, 27, 28\}$ and all $m \ge 30$, requiring primarily a non-zero leading coefficient. Since the underlying evaluation coefficients must be determined by solving systems of nonlinear equations (SNEs), selecting a numerically stable solution set is critical. We introduce an automated verification routine designed to filter and validate robust coefficient sets for floating-point execution. The primary contribution is a MATLAB implementation leveraging variable precision arithmetic to handle the underlying SNEs, verify stability, and project precision bounds. Numerical experiments involving various matrix functions verify that the developed implementation preserves or, in some instances, enhances the numerical accuracy of the classic PS method, while systematically achieving the theoretical reduction of $2M$.

</details>


### [132] [Galerkin method for asymmetrically-weighted Hermite approximations applied to the Vlasov-Poisson system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28409)
*Ruiyang Dai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究使用非对称加权(AW)Hermite基函数在速度空间中的Vlasov-Poisson(VP)系统的数值方法。作者分析了AW Hermite谱形式不稳定的原因，提出采用Galerkin方法替代经典的Petrov-Galerkin方法以保证L2范数稳定性，并给出了计算成本相当但更稳定的等效形式，最后通过数值模拟验证了该方法的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VP系统是一个双曲型系统，使用非对称加权Hermite基函数的谱方法在速度空间求解时存在稳定性问题。经典Petrov-Galerkin方法无法自然保证L2稳定性，需要寻求一种能确保L2范数稳定性的数值方案。

Method: 本文采用Galerkin方法替代Petrov-Galerkin方法，以自然确保L2范数下的稳定性；同时提出该方法的等效形式，在保持计算成本适中的前提下实现稳定求解。

Result: 基于所提出的Hermite谱方法进行了数值模拟，结果表明该方法具有良好的稳定性。

Conclusion: Galerkin型Hermite谱方法能够有效解决AW Hermite基在VP系统求解中的不稳定问题，在保持计算效率的同时确保了数值稳定性。

Abstract: We investigate a numerical method for the Vlasov-Poisson (VP) system utilizing asymmetrically-weighted (AW) Hermite bases in velocity space, which is an hyperbolic system. In particular, we concentrate on spectral methods in velocity. For the Hermite spectral form of the VP system, we analyze the resaon that the form with AW Hermite bases can be instable. To obtain L2 stability properties, we consider a Galerkin method intead of the classical Petrov-Galerkin method, which naturally ensures stability with respect to the L2 norm. We also present an equivalent form of the method that maintains a computational cost modest compared to that of the Petrov-Galerkin method. Finally, we present numerical simulations based on the proposed Hermite spectral method, showcasing its stability.

</details>


### [133] [Windowed thinning and query complexity for the bouncy particle and Zigzag samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28413)
*Jianfeng Lu,Yinchen Luo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于窗口细化(windowed thinning)的精确模拟方法，用于弹跳粒子采样器(BPS)和坐标Zigzag过程，在强凸且光滑的目标分布下给出了从高斯冷启动出发的总变差误差ε的梯度查询复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹跳粒子采样器(BPS)和Zigzag过程是高效的概率度量模拟方法，但其事件率的精确模拟具有挑战性。现有方法在理论保证方面有限，特别是缺少从冷启动出发的有限时间查询复杂度分析。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供明确的可扩展性保证。

Method: 将轨迹划分为确定性窗口，在每个窗口开始时只做一次梯度评估，构建事件率的可处理局部包络(local envelope)；结合定量混合估计以及弹跳次数和翻转次数的有限时间界，推导从高斯冷启动出发的查询复杂度。

Result: 在总变差误差ε下，BPS的期望梯度查询数为O(κ^{1/2}d(d log κ + log(1/ε)))，Zigzag的期望全梯度等价查询数为O(κ d^{1/4}(d log κ + log(1/ε)))，其中d个坐标偏导查询计为一次等价。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为BPS和Zigzag提供了从高斯冷启动出发的有限时间精确模拟查询复杂度保证，揭示了二者在条件数κ和维度d方面的不同依赖关系，为实际算法选择提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Let $μ(d x)\propto e^{-U(x)} d x$ on $\R^d$, where $U$ is $m$-strongly convex and $L$-smooth, and denote by $κ=L/m$ the condition number. We consider windowed thinning, an exact simulation method for the bouncy particle sampler and the coordinate Zigzag process. The method divides a trajectory into deterministic windows and uses a gradient evaluation at the beginning of each window to construct a tractable local envelope for the event rate. Combining this construction with quantitative mixing estimates and finite-time bounds on the expected numbers of bounces and flips yields query complexity guarantees from a Gaussian cold start. For total-variation error $\varepsilon$, the expected query counts are $O(κ^{1/2}d\,(d\logκ+\log\frac1\varepsilon))$ gradient queries for the bouncy particle sampler and $O(κd^{1/4}(d\logκ+\log\frac1\varepsilon))$ full-gradient equivalents for Zigzag, where $d$ coordinate-partial queries count as one equivalent.

</details>


### [134] [Graph Neural Multilevel Preconditioners for Iterative Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28456)
*Zechen Zhang,Rui Peng Li,Yousef Saad*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出图神经多级预条件子（GMP），将代数多重网格（AMG）的层级结构作为先验，在一个统一框架中学习光滑、限制和插值算子，用于一般稀疏线性系统的Krylov迭代求解器。在超过800个稀疏矩阵上的基准测试中，与经典AMG、ILUT及最先进的GNN预条件子进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典AMG方法在椭圆型PDE启发下发展，对不定或非对称系统的鲁棒性可能退化；现有GNN预条件子虽然具有数据驱动优势，但其在一般稀疏矩阵上强制增加AMG式层级结构的实际影响尚未被充分探索。因此需要研究将AMG层级结构作为先验融入GNN学习框架的效果。

Method: 提出Graph Neural Multilevel Preconditioner (GMP)：采用AMG层级结构作为结构先验，在统一框架中同时学习三个关键算子——光滑算子（smoother）、限制算子（restriction）和插值算子（interpolation），并作为可直接嵌入标准Krylov求解器的即插即用预条件子。

Result: 在超过800个稀疏矩阵的基准上，与经典AMG、单层ILUT及最先进的GNN预条件子进行了系统对比，刻画了多层级图神经网络预条件在哪些条件下能改善收敛性，以及相对于强单层基线在哪些情况下会引入额外的计算开销。

Conclusion: 结果既展示了AMG式多层结构在学得预条件子中的潜力，也揭示了其局限性：强制施加多层结构并非总能优于强单层基线，需要根据具体问题特性权衡利弊。

Abstract: Solving large, sparse linear systems is a core task in scientific computing, and efficient iterative solvers rely critically on effective and robust preconditioning. While classical methods such as algebraic multigrid (AMG) are highly scalable, their robustness can degrade on indefinite or nonsymmetric systems where heuristics originally developed for elliptic PDEs are less reliable. Recently, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as data-driven preconditioners; yet, the practical impact of imposing an AMG-style hierarchy remains underexplored for general sparse matrices. In this work, we propose a Graph Neural Multilevel Preconditioner (GMP) that adopts an AMG hierarchy as a structural prior and learns smoothing, restriction, and interpolation operators in a unified framework. Our method targets general sparse systems and is instantiated as a drop-in preconditioner for standard Krylov solvers. On a benchmark of over 800 sparse matrices, we compare against classical AMG, single-level ILUT, and state-of-the-art GNN preconditioners, and characterize the regimes where multilevel graph neural preconditioning improves convergence or, conversely, introduces overhead relative to strong single-level baselines. These results highlight both the promise and the limitations of enforcing AMG-style multilevel structure in learned preconditioners for large-scale scientific simulations.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

### [135] [Clifford-Appell formulation of a Dirac-type Kronig-Penney model in condensed matter physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27920)
*L. Aceto,P. A. Grassi,H. Malonek*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究(N+1)维Clifford分析中广义Kronig-Penney模型的稳态Dirac方程，通过引入广义Cauchy-Riemann算子将自由Dirac系统重述为一阶耦合系统，并借助特征超复变量将其约化为解耦的广义Helmholtz方程。通过构造双变量Clifford-Appell多项式基，将微分问题转化为代数递推关系，得到解的显式级数表示，建立了超复函数论中广义Helmholtz方程与Appell多项式系统之间的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Kronig-Penney模型研究多局限于一维或整数维空间，缺乏在高维Clifford分析框架下的统一处理方法；同时自由Dirac系统的传播问题与广义Helmholtz方程及Appell多项式系统之间的内在联系尚未被系统阐明。

Method: 在远离相互作用位点处引入广义Cauchy-Riemann算子将自由Dirac系统重述为两个Clifford值场的耦合一阶系统；利用特征超复变量将该系统约化为一对解耦的广义Helmholtz方程；构造适应广义Cauchy-Riemann算子的双变量Clifford-Appell多项式基，将微分问题通过Appell展开转化为展开系数的代数递推关系，从而获得解的显式级数表示。

Result: 该框架为自由传播问题提供了统一的Clifford-解析表述，建立了广义Helmholtz方程与超复函数论中Appell多项式系统之间的显式联系，并给出了稳态Dirac方程解的显式级数表示。

Conclusion: 这项工作在Clifford分析框架下统一了Kronig-Penney模型中自由Dirac方程的求解方法，拓展了Appell多项式在超复偏微分方程求解中的应用，为后续研究含相互作用项的完整Kronig-Penney模型奠定了数学基础。

Abstract: We investigate the stationary Dirac equation associated with a generalized Kronig-Penney model in (N+1)-dimensional Clifford analysis. Away from the interaction sites, the free Dirac system is reformulated by introducing suitable generalized Cauchy-Riemann operators, leading to a coupled first-order system for two Clifford-valued fields. By means of characteristic hypercomplex variables, this system is reduced to a pair of decoupled generalized Helmholtz equations. To construct explicit solutions, we introduce a bivariate Clifford-Appell polynomial basis adapted to the generalized Cauchy-Riemann operators. The Appell expansions transform the differential problem into an algebraic recurrence for the expansion coefficients, yielding explicit series representations of the solutions. The proposed framework provides a unified Clifford-analytic formulation of the free propagation problem and establishes an explicit connection between generalized Helmholtz equations, and Appell polynomial systems in hypercomplex function theory.

</details>


### [136] [Consistent symmetry breaking and topological phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28181)
*Yuezhao Li,Guo Chuan Thiang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用对称性破缺下等变指标的一致性原理，建立了等变指标的规范弱/强二分法，并探讨了其与粗几何（宏观）指标的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对称群作用下，算子在某些等变指标上必须满足一致性约束。该原则源于凝聚态物理中拓扑绝缘体的理论，作者希望将其形式化并建立规范性的弱/强二分法。

Method: 利用"在对称群下不变的算子在任意有限指标子群下也不变"这一基本事实，结合等变指标在对称性破缺（即限制到有限指标子群）时的一致性，构建弱/强等变指标的二分法框架，并与粗几何（宏观）指标建立联系。

Result: 建立了等变指标的规范弱/强二分法，验证了等变指标与粗几何/宏观指标之间的关联性。

Conclusion: 对称性破缺的一致性原理为等变指标的分类提供了系统性框架，且该框架与粗几何指标存在深层联系，为拓扑不变量理论提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Operators invariant under a symmetry group are also invariant under any finite-index subgroup. The equivariant indices of such operators must be consistent under symmetry breaking. We use this principle to establish a canonical weak/strong dichotomy for equivariant indices, building on an idea originating in the theory of topological insulators in solid-state physics. We also study the relationship to coarse-geometric, or macroscopic, indices.

</details>


### [137] [The reduced Dirac structure of General Relativity on manifolds with corners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28262)
*Alberto S. Cattaneo,Filippo Fila-Robattino,Manuel Tecchiolli*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了四维Palatini-Cartan引力中角（corner）泊松结构。基于流形带边界引力理论的经典描述（特别是边界约束代数），在角场空间上获得了一个pre-Dirac结构，并通过约化程序得到最大Dirac结构（即泊松双矢量场的图）。该泊松结构被证明具有等价的仿射泊松描述，自然地展示了约化角理论为类BF理论，导致BF²V形式化，为Palatini-Cartan引力的体、边界和角结构的统一框架提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力在于构建四维引力理论中层叠（bulk-surface-corner）结构的统一描述框架，特别是厘清角（corner）层面的辛/泊松几何结构及其与边界约束代数的关系。

Method: 采用经典约束代数分析，在角场空间上构造pre-Dirac结构，通过约化程序提取最大Dirac结构，将其识别为泊松双矢量场的图；随后证明该结构等价的仿射泊松表述，并由此导出BF类理论及BF²V形式化。

Result: 成功获得角场约化空间上的泊松结构，揭示其等价的仿射泊松形式，并实现了角理论与BF类理论的关联，建立了BF²V表述。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了四维Palatini-Cartan引力中体、边界与角结构的统一泊松几何框架，为后续量子化或进一步几何量化提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: In this paper, the corner Poisson structure of four-dimensional Palatini-Cartan gravity is derived. Building on the classical description of gravity on manifolds with boundary, specifically on the boundary constraint algebra, a pre- Dirac structure on the space of corner fields is obtained together with a reduction procedure that yields a maximal Dirac structure, identified as the graph of a Poisson bivector field, on the reduced space of corner fields. It is further shown that this Poisson structure admits an equivalent affine Poisson description, which naturally exhibits the reduced corner theory as a $BF$-like theory and leads to a BF$^2$V formulation. This provides the basis for a unified framework for the bulk, boundary, and corner structures of Palatini-Cartan gravity.

</details>


### [138] [Integrability in Asymptotic Symmetries of Spacetime: the $\text{BMS}_3$ scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28454)
*Corentin Vitel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过多种结构方法重新审视了Fuentealba等人(2018)提出的bms3可积层次。利用变分复形构造了bms3双哈密顿结构并附加Nijenhuis算子；对AdS3情形做了类似论证并验证其平坦极限恢复渐近平坦情形；提出τ-方案描述；通过Lie-Poisson描述表明该层次不唯一；并证明一类能量依赖的Schrödinger算子的Lax流由bms3的余伴随轨道描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望从更系统的结构角度（变分复形、双哈密顿结构、Nijenhuis算子、Lie-Poisson描述等）重新验证和深化bms3可积层次的理论框架，并探讨其与AdS3情形的联系以及层次的可能推广。

Method: 本文采用多种数学结构方法：(1) 在多项式符号环上的变分复形中构造bms3双哈密顿结构；(2) 附加Nijenhuis算子；(3) 对AdS3情形进行类似构造并检验其平坦极限；(4) 提出替代的τ-方案描述；(5) 利用Lie-Poisson结构讨论层次的非唯一性；(6) 对能量依赖的Schrödinger算子类，建立其Lax流与bms3余伴随轨道之间的联系。

Result: 成功构建了bms3双哈密顿结构及其Nijenhuis算子；确认AdS3构造的平坦极限与渐近平坦情形一致；证明了τ-方案描述的有效性；通过Lie-Poisson分析揭示了bms3层次并非唯一；建立了能量依赖Schrödinger算子的Lax流与bms3余伴随轨道之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 本文以更严格的结构化方法巩固并拓展了bms3可积层次的理论基础，明确了其双哈密顿结构、平坦极限关系、非唯一性以及Lax流与余伴随轨道的联系，为后续研究提供了更完整的数学框架。

Abstract: We revisit the proof of a $\mathfrak{bms}_3-$integrable hierarchy introduced in Fuentealba et al. (JHEP, 2018), using different structural methodologies. Specifically, from the variational complex on the ring of polynomial symbols, a $\mathfrak{bms}_3$ bi-Hamiltonian structure is constructed on which a Nijenhuis operator can be attached. A similar argument is made for the $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ case, where we check that the flat limit of the latter recovers the asymptotically flat situation. An alternative $τ-$scheme description is also presented. Moreover, a Lie-Poisson description suggests that such a $\mathfrak{bms}_3-$hierarchy is not unique. For a subclass of so-called energy-dependent Schrödinger operators, it is shown that their Lax flows are described by the coadjoint orbits of $\mathfrak{bms}_3$.

</details>


### [139] [Redundant moments of non-melonic random tensor models: simplifying the tensor bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28518)
*Carlos I. Perez-Sanchez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在任意张量模型中，大N极限下的"melon族"矩具有冗余性，即任何melon算子C在大N下的期望值仅取决于C的degree，这一性质同样适用于melon矩的Schwinger-Dyson方程。该结论大幅减少了张量bootstrap核心中正半定矩阵的独立条目数量，且适用范围不限于melon张量模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量bootstrap是研究张量模型的重要工具，其核心涉及正半定矩阵（类似于格点规范理论和随机矩阵bootstrap中的Toeplitz和Hankel矩阵的推广）。然而这些矩阵包含大量独立条目，计算复杂度高，需要寻找冗余关系来简化问题。

Method: 作者在大N极限下证明了melon族矩的冗余性：任何melon算子C的期望值仅通过其degree依赖于C。同时证明该冗余性对melon矩的Schwinger-Dyson方程同样成立，从而缩减了张量bootstrap中正半定矩阵的独立元素数量。

Result: 证明了melon族算子在大N下的冗余性定理，即期望值仅依赖degree，并推广至Schwinger-Dyson方程；其结果适用范围不仅限于melon张量模型，具有广泛性。

Conclusion: 该结果有效减少了张量bootstrap中正半定矩阵的独立条目数，提升了计算效率，且结论的普适性使其对更广泛的张量模型具有应用价值。

Abstract: We prove the redundancy of a family (the so-called melonic family) of large-$N$ moments in arbitrary tensor models. This reduces the number of independent entries in the positive semidefinite matrices that build the core of the tensor bootstrap (for tensor models there are already several generalizations of the positive semidefinite Toeplitz and Hankel matrices of lattice gauge theory and random matrix bootstrap, respectively). More concretely, we prove that any melonic operator $C$ at large-$N$ has an expectation value that depends on $C$ exclusively through its degree. A similar statement is shown here to hold for the Schwinger-Dyson equations of melonic moments. The strength of our result is also its scope, which is not limited to melonic tensor models.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Minimum-Width Drawing of Trees with Sized Vertices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27445)
*Markus Wallinger,Oscar Navarro,Stephen G. Kobourov*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 研究带矩形顶点（给定宽度和高度）的有根树的层次化布局问题MWD，允许对每个内部顶点的子节点进行重新排序以最小化布局宽度。问题被证明是NP完全的（即使是单位宽度的二叉树），论文提出了精确求解的混合整数线性规划（MILP）和快速启发式算法，实验表明重新排序可使布局宽度中位数减少约20%，单个实例最多减少约55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中树顶点需要用给定宽高的矩形而非点来表示，且许多场景中（如无固定语义顺序的应用）子节点的顺序可以自由选择。合理选择子节点顺序能够显著减少绘图的宽度，但这一问题缺乏系统研究，论文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 论文形式化定义了MWD问题（给定有根树及顶点宽度，为每个内部顶点寻找子节点顺序以最小化层次化布局宽度）。理论方面，证明了问题在单位宽度的二叉树情况下也是NP完全的；算法方面，提出了一个精确求解的混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型用于中等规模实例，以及一个快速启发式算法用于实践中的大规模场景。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上评估两种方法并与基线对比：重新排序使布局宽度中位数减少约20%，最多减少约55%。启发式算法在1秒内计算完成布局，且在MILP证明最优性的情况下，四分之三的实例中启发式解与最优宽度的差距在25%以内。

Conclusion: MWD问题虽然理论上是NP完全的，但通过精确的MILP求解和快速启发式算法能够在实践中有效解决；重新排序子节点能显著降低树的层次化布局宽度，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Trees arise in many applications and computing nice tree layouts is a classical problem in information visualization. In many practical settings, vertices need to be represented as rectangles with a given width and height rather than as points. When an order over the children of each vertex is given, polynomial-time algorithms are known that produce drawings adhering to various drawing conventions. However, in many applications, the order of children carries no semantic meaning, and choosing it well can significantly reduce the drawing's width. In this paper, we study the problem \textsc{Min-Width Tree Drawing with Reordering} (\textsc{MWD}): given a rooted tree whose vertices have prescribed widths, find a sibling order at each internal vertex that minimizes the width of the resulting layered drawing. We show that the problem is \textsf{NP}-complete, even on binary trees with unit-width vertices. We present a mixed integer linear program that solves \textsc{MWD} exactly on moderately sized instances, and a heuristic that is fast and delivers good results in practice. We evaluate both approaches against a baseline on synthetic and real-world datasets, where reordering reduces drawing width by a median of $\approx20\%$ and by up to $\approx55\%$ on individual instances. The heuristic computes its layouts in under a second and, when the MILP proves optimality, it stays within $25\%$ of the optimal width in three-quarters of all instances.

</details>


### [141] [Tight UGC Thresholds for Geometric Stabbing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28062)
*Khaled Elbassioni,Rishikesh Gajjala,Saurabh Ray*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意固定、有界arity的积分间隙覆盖LP实例在UGC下具有匹配的硬度比，并通过随机舍入和全支撑扰动构造连通局部分布，从而为三类几何stabbing问题（轴平行d方体、水平线段、分离d区间横向）确定了紧的UGC阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多几何stabbing问题自然地归结为覆盖LP，其中每个约束是候选有序集上连续迹的并集。此前这些问题的常数近似比与UGC硬度之间缺乏系统性联系，需要一个传递定理将LP积分间隙转化为UGC下的近似硬度，以判定这些问题的近似阈值是否紧。

Method: 证明了一个传递定理：任意固定、有限且有界arity的此类积分间隙实例在UGC下产生匹配的硬度比。构造使用Kumar等人（SODA 2011）的严格CSP框架，通过随机舍入（按边缘概率x_i选择候选i）和全支撑扰动来构建所需的连通局部分布，使得每个连续迹T被击中的概率为min{1,x(T)}。

Result: 得到三个紧UGC阈值：(1) 对任意固定d≥2，用坐标超平面刺穿任意大小轴平行d方体，阈值为d（d=2时矩形和正方形stabbing阈值为2，匹配Gaur等人的2-近似）；(2) 用水平和垂直线刺穿水平线段，阈值为e/(e-1)，匹配Kovaleva和Spieksma的近似比；(3) 分离d区间横向问题的阈值为d，补上了Ben-David等人d-近似在UGC下的缺口。

Conclusion: 该传递定理统一了多类几何stabbing问题的UGC紧硬度结果，证明这些问题的LP松弛均是紧的，所有已知近似算法在UGC下均是最优的，从而完整解决了这些问题的近似阈值判定。

Abstract: Many geometric stabbing problems admit natural covering LPs in which each constraint is a union of consecutive traces on ordered candidate sets. We prove a transfer theorem showing that every fixed finite, bounded-arity integrality-gap instance of this form yields a matching hardness ratio under the Unique Games Conjecture. Using the strict-CSP framework of Kumar, Manokaran, Tulsiani, and Vishnoi [SODA 2011], we construct the required connected local distributions by randomized rounding and a full-support perturbation. Given a fractional vector $x$ on a block, the rounding selects candidate $i$ with marginal probability $x_i$ and hits each consecutive trace $T$ with probability $\min\{1,x(T)\}$.
  We obtain three tight UGC thresholds. First, for every fixed $d\ge 2$, stabbing arbitrary-size axis-parallel $d$-cubes with coordinate hyperplanes has threshold $d$. For $d=2$, the hardness holds for arbitrary-size squares and establishes threshold $2$ for rectangle and square stabbing, matching the $2$-approximation of Gaur, Ibaraki, and Krishnamurti [ESA 2000]. Second, stabbing horizontal segments with horizontal and vertical lines has threshold $e/(e-1)$, matching the $e/(e-1)$-approximation of Kovaleva and Spieksma [ESA 2004]. Third, separated $d$-interval transversal has threshold $d$ for every fixed $d\ge 2$, closing under UGC the gap left by the $d$-approximation of Ben-David, Grant, Ma, and Sharpe [CCCG 2012].

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Entropy-Smooth Convex Optimization Cannot Be Accelerated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27476)
*Jacob M. Aguirre,Dmitrii M. Ostrovskii*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在标准d-单纯形上、关于负熵L-光滑的凸函数类中，任何一阶方法的收敛率下界为Ω(L/T)，当d=Ω(T²)时成立。这表明镜像下降在该函数类中是最优的（至多差一个对数因子）。作者还将结果扩展到量子设定，即关于负冯·诺依曼熵L-光滑的函数类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Dragomir等人已证明相对光滑条件下加速可能是不可能的，但他们的代理函数（prox-function）是人为构造的且与困难实例一起设计，缺乏实际意义。作者希望针对具有特别良好结构的代理函数（负熵）证明非加速性，以说明此前的悲观结论并非仅仅源于病态的构造。

Method: 作者为凸且关于负熵L-光滑的函数类构造了匹配下界的困难实例，利用d=Ω(T²)的高维条件建立第一类一阶方法的收敛率下界Ω(L/T)。同时将传统欧几里得框架下的论证推广到量子设定，针对量子态（厄米半正定单位迹矩阵）构成的谱面体，使用负冯·诺依曼熵作为Bregman散度。

Result: 证明了在第一类一阶方法中，对于凸且关于负熵L-光滑的函数类，收敛率下界为Ω(L/T)，说明加速（即O(1/T²)阶）在此类函数中不可能实现，镜像下降已是最优（差分对数因子）。该结果同样适用于量子设定下关于负冯·诺依曼熵L-光滑的函数类。

Conclusion: 即使代理函数具有良好结构（负熵），相对于熵的L-光滑凸优化中仍无法实现加速；这一结果同时适用于经典单纯形和量子谱面体两个设定，并表明镜像下降在这类问题中本质上是最优算法。

Abstract: We prove an $Ω(L/T)$ lower bound for the convergence rate of minimization in the class of functions that are convex and $L$-smooth relative to negative entropy on the standard $d$-simplex, valid for every first-order method when $d = Ω(T^2)$. In particular, this shows that mirror descent is optimal up to a logarithmic factor in this class. This may be surprising due to the fact that accelerated methods are readily available under the assumption of smoothness in $\ell_1$-norm. While Dragomir et al. (Mathematical Programming, 2022) have already showed that acceleration might be impossible under relative smoothness, their prox-function is pathological and constructed together with the hard instance. In contrast, we show non-acceleration for a specific prox-function with particularly favorable structure. We also extend the result to the quantum setting, proving the same lower bound in the class of functions $L$-smooth relative to negative von Neumann entropy on the spectrahedron of $d \times d$ Hermitian positive-semidefinite matrices with unit trace.

</details>


### [143] [Branching out: Prognostics-Based Replacement Policies for Series Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27899)
*Daniel Koutas,Daniel Straub*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于预测性维护的混合规划方法，通过考虑决策选项、预后模型信息和更新-奖励过程成本，生成参数化维护策略，在预防性更换和预防性订购两个场景中与优化基准策略性能相当，但更高效且对过拟合更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测性维护策略通常需要复杂的优化过程，可能面临过拟合问题且计算效率较低。作者旨在提出一种更简洁、高效的策略生成方法，在保持性能的同时提升鲁棒性和可解释性。

Method: 提出一种混合规划方法，该方法同时考虑：(1) 可用的决策选项；(2) 由预后模型提供的系统未来状态信息；(3) 底层更新-奖励过程的成本。由此产生的策略仅由少量参数定义，这些参数可通过理论推导或基于运行至失效数据的优化来确定。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法在预防性更换和预防性订购两个决策场景中，其性能可与优化后的基准策略相媲美，同时显著提高了计算效率，并且对过拟合具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该混合规划方法为预测性维护策略设计提供了一种兼具性能与效率的可行方案，其参数化的策略形式使得策略易于实施和调整。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid planning method for deriving prognostics-based predictive maintenance policies. The method accounts for the available decision options, the information on the future state of the system provided by a prognostic model, and the costs of the underlying renewal-reward process. It results in policies defined by only a few parameters, which can be determined based on theoretical considerations or by optimization from run-to-failure data. We demonstrate the potential of the method in two separate predictive maintenance decision settings: preventive replacement and preventive ordering. Numerical investigations show that the derived policies rival the performance of optimized benchmark policies, while being significantly more efficient and robust against overfitting.

</details>


### [144] [The Existence and Stability of Generalized Multi-Source Weber Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27900)
*V. S. T. Long,N. M. Nam,T. H. Tai,C. Tammer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在最小时间函数框架下研究具有集值目标的一般化多源Weber问题，建立了全局与局部最优解的存在性及解集性质，推导了目标函数与最优值函数的Lipschitz连续性，并分析了全局和局部解映射的稳定性，提供了该问题在集合扰动下的定量与定性敏感性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Weber问题通常假设目标为单点集，但在实际应用中目标可能具有不确定性或集值结构。本文旨在将多源Weber问题推广到集值目标设置下，并在最小时间函数的框架下系统研究其解的存在性、解集结构以及解对目标集扰动的稳定性，从而弥补该领域在定量和定性敏感性分析方面的空白。

Method: 本文采用最小时间函数作为距离度量工具来刻画目标集与决策点之间的联系。方法上首先通过变分分析和优化理论建立全局与局部最优解的存在性条件；其次利用集值分析中的收敛概念，推导目标函数和最优值函数关于目标集扰动的Lipschitz连续性；最后引入全局与局部解映射，并运用集值映射的上半连续性、下半连续性等稳定性工具对其进行分析。

Result: 获得了以下主要结果：(1) 证明了全局和局部最优解的存在性；(2) 刻画了解集的闭性、紧致性以及有界/无界的条件；(3) 建立了目标函数和最优值函数关于目标集扰动的显式Lipschitz连续性；(4) 给出了全局和局部解映射的稳定性性质。

Conclusion: 该研究为一般化多源Weber问题在最小时间函数框架下提供了完整的定量与定性敏感性分析，为后续在鲁棒选址、最优运输等实际应用中的进一步研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the generalized multi-source Weber problem with set-valued targets in the framework of minimal time functions. We first establish the existence of global and local optimal solutions and investigate several qualitative properties of the corresponding solution sets, including closedness, compactness, and conditions ensuring boundedness or unboundedness. Next, we derive explicit Lipschitz continuity properties for the objective function and the associated optimal value function with respect to perturbations of the target sets. We then introduce the global and local solution mappings and study their stability properties from the viewpoint of set-valued analysis. These results provide a quantitative and qualitative sensitivity analysis for the generalized multi-source Weber problem in the setting of minimal time functions.

</details>


### [145] [OptGraph: Large Language Models Enhanced Evolutionary Optimization Via Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27918)
*Xianchao Xiu,Jianhao Li,Huangyue Chen,Wanquan Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: OptGraph是一种引入图检索增强生成（GraphRAG）的优化智能体工作流，通过构建可复用经验的有向图来捕获建模模式、问题形式化、实现细节与错误修正之间的关系，在推理阶段利用图邻域信息增强检索知识，并支持自适应知识更新，在基准数据集上平均精确度比最先进的基于提示的自动优化框架高8.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动进化优化方法存在三个关键局限：模式复用能力不足（缺乏可复用的结构化经验）、错误感知修正能力弱（无法有效利用验证反馈）、检索鲁棒性差（跨任务知识检索不稳定）。而图结构作为关系建模工具，能够更好地捕捉优化组件之间的复杂关联，为突破这些限制提供了新思路。

Method: OptGraph的核心创新在于将GraphRAG引入优化智能体工作流。具体步骤：(1) 将可复用经验构建为带类型的图结构，编码建模模式、问题形式化、实现细节与错误修正之间的多元关系；(2) 在推理阶段，利用图邻域信息丰富检索到的知识，为建模、验证与迭代优化提供结构化上下文；(3) 支持自适应知识更新，通过蒸馏执行轨迹和验证反馈，将经验增量转化为图知识，无需对LLM参数进行微调。

Result: 在基准数据集上进行的大量实验表明，OptGraph相比最先进的基于提示的自动优化框架，平均精确度提升了8.9%。代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: OptGraph通过图结构化的知识表示与检索增强机制，有效提升了LLM自动优化在多任务场景下的模式复用能力、错误感知修正能力和检索鲁棒性，为进化优化与LLM智能体的结合提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful tool for automated evolutionary optimization, but existing methods remain limited in pattern reuse, error-aware refinement, and retrieval robustness across diverse tasks. To address these limitations, we propose OptGraph, the first optimization agentic workflow that introduces graph retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG). Specifically, OptGraph first constructs reusable experience as a typed graph, capturing the relationships among modeling patterns, problem formalization, implementation details, and error corrections. In the inference stage, OptGraph leverages graph neighborhood information to enrich retrieved knowledge, providing structured context to improve modeling, verification, and iterative refinement. Moreover, OptGraph supports adaptive knowledge updates, enabling the distillation of execution traces and verification feedback into reusable graph knowledge without ndertaking LLM parameter tuning. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets show that our proposed OptGraph achieves an average exact accuracy 8.9% higher than the state-of-the-art prompt-based automated optimization frameworks. Our code has been made available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/OptGraph.

</details>


### [146] [Global exponential turnpike properties for optimal control of the viscous Burgers equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28042)
*Emmanuel Trélat,Xingwu Zeng,Can Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一维粘性Burgers方程受局部内部控制约束的二次最优跟踪问题，建立了全局指数turnpike性质。对任意初始数据，有限时域最优解在周期跟踪目标足够小时逼近唯一的周期最优状态；零目标情形下则在原点处建立全局稳态turnpike，且对初始数据无小性假设。这是粘性Burgers方程的首个全局指数turnpike结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: turnpike性质（最优解在大部分时间接近稳态或周期稳态）在最优控制理论中具有重要地位，但针对非线性偏微分方程（特别是Burgers方程）的全局指数turnpike结果此前尚属空白。已有工作多限于线性系统或局部结果，需要推广到非线性粘性Burgers方程并去除初始数据的限制。

Method: 证明分两步结合：(1) 通过严格凸性和周期Riccati理论建立局部指数turnpike性质；(2) 利用抛物型耗散机制构造一个与时间区间长度无关的吸引时间（absorbing time），使得系统在有限时间内进入局部turnpike区域，从而将局部结果提升为全局结果。针对零目标情形，进一步利用抛物型方程本身的耗散性消除初始数据的小性假设。

Result: 对一维粘性Burgers方程，得到了全局指数turnpike性质。周期跟踪情形下，若目标足够小，任意初始数据的最优解指数逼近唯一周期最优轨道；零目标情形下，所有初始数据的最优解全局指数逼近原点稳态，无需小性假设。

Conclusion: 该结果为粘性Burgers方程最优控制提供了首个全局指数turnpike理论，证明了局部凸性分析与抛物耗散估计结合可有效推广turnpike性质到非线性系统，为后续处理更广泛的非线性偏微分方程最优控制问题提供了方法论参考。

Abstract: We establish global exponential turnpike properties for quadratic optimal tracking problems governed by the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation with localized internal control. For every initial datum, finite-horizon optimal solutions approach the unique optimal periodic regime when the periodic tracking target is sufficiently small; the zero-target case yields a global steady turnpike at the origin, with no smallness assumption on the initial datum. To our knowledge, these are the first global exponential turnpike results for the viscous Burgers equation. The proof combines a local exponential turnpike, obtained through strict convexity and periodic Riccati theory, with a parabolic dissipation argument that provides an absorbing time independent of the horizon.

</details>


### [147] [Multi-Asset Utility Maximization with Jump Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28051)
*Sigui Brice Dro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多维布朗运动与独立齐次泊松随机测度驱动的风险资产市场中，允许策略包含跳跃信号的组合效用最大化问题。通过半鞅过程表示组合动态，利用鞅最优性原理推导出带跳的倒向随机微分方程（BSDE），证明了其解的存在唯一性，并探讨了异质性跳跃信号情形，最后以高斯信号为例提供了对数效用情形的数值示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将一维指数效用情形下的组合优化方法推广到更一般的多维布朗运动与跳跃过程驱动的市场环境，并允许投资者在策略中利用跳跃信号，以更贴近实际金融市场中资产价格存在跳扩散特征的需求。

Method: 首先将组合动态表示为半鞅过程；然后运用鞅最优性原理推导出相应的带跳BSDE，用于刻画值函数和最优策略；接着证明该带跳BSDE解的存在性与唯一性；此外讨论了投资者收到异质跳跃信号的特殊情形；最后针对对数效用情形提供带高斯信号的数值模拟。

Result: 成功推导出多维跳跃市场下组合效用最大化对应的带跳BSDE，证明了其解的存在唯一性，并给出异质跳跃信号情形的处理方法以及高斯信号下对数效用情形的数值示例。

Conclusion: 该研究将跳跃信号纳入多维跳扩散市场的组合最优化框架中，通过带跳BSDE统一刻画了值函数与最优策略，为考虑跳跃信息的投资决策提供了理论基础和数值方法支撑。

Abstract: In this paper, we study portfolio utility maximization problem in a setting where the risky asset is driven by a multidimensional Brownian motion and an independent homogeneous Poisson random measure, and where strategies may incorporate jump signals. Following the same approach as in the one-dimensional case for the exponential utility function [17], we first represent the portfolio dynamics as semimartingale processes. We then use martingale optimality principle to derive the corresponding backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE) with jumps to characterize both value function and an optimal strategy in terms of its solution. We subsequently prove the existence and uniqueness of the solution to the related BSDE with jumps. We also address the idiosyncratic case i.e where the investor receive heterogeneous jump signals. Finally, we provide numerical illustrations with Gaussian signals for the logarithmic case.

</details>


### [148] [On Leader Selection for Strong Structural Controllability in Matrix-Weighted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28168)
*Lanhao Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于矩阵加权网络中最小领导者集选择的严格数学框架，以解决强结构可控性（SSC）的NP-hard逆综合问题。通过证明结构不可控性源于维度特异性可达性隔离和拓扑对称等价性，设计了包含可达性先决条件和三种对称破缺算法的两阶段综合方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵加权网络中保证强结构可控性的最小领导者集选择问题是一个尚未解决的NP-hard难题，现有方法无法有效应对这一挑战。

Method: 采用两阶段综合方法：第一阶段为可达性先决条件，用于识别结构根节点；第二阶段包含三种对称破缺算法——贪心Weisfeiler-Lehman选择、子模界最大化和分区熵最大化。

Result: 通过数学证明保证了算法对不变子空间和结构扩张的免疫性，并在多种拓扑结构上进行了广泛的数值评估验证。

Conclusion: 该框架严格解决了矩阵加权网络中的最小领导者集选择问题，为SSC提供了可证明有效的求解方法。

Abstract: The inverse synthesis problem of selecting a minimal leader set to guarantee strong structural controllability (SSC) in matrix-weighted networks remains an unresolved NP-hard challenge. This paper proposes a rigorous mathematical framework to solve this. We prove that structural uncontrollability stems exclusively from dimension-specific reachability isolation and topological symmetry equivalence. To overcome these bottlenecks, we formulate a two-phase synthesis: a reachability prerequisite to identify structural roots, followed by three distinct symmetry-breaking algorithms (Greedy Weisfeiler-Lehman Selection, Submodular Bound Maximization, and Partition Entropy Maximization). Mathematical proofs guarantee immunity to invariant subspaces and structural dilation, validated by extensive numerical evaluations across diverse topologies.

</details>


### [149] [The optimality of an (s, S) hiring policy on a workforce planning problem with fixed recruitment costs and binomial turnover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28171)
*Zhen Chen,Roberto Rossi,Belen Martin-Barragan,S. Armagan Tarim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域的员工配置规划问题，其中每期员工流失服从依赖于招聘后员工规模的二项分布，并引入固定招聘成本。通过建立变量单期成本的离散凸性和期望总成本的K-凸性，提出二项K-凸性概念，证明最优招聘策略具有(s,S)型结构，并开发分段逼近方法得到可高效求解的混合整数线性规划模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统劳动力规划模型往往假设员工流失率独立于当前员工规模，且忽略固定招聘成本。现实中，招聘行为伴随固定成本，而员工流失率与在职人数密切相关，这导致决策依赖的状态转移概率使标准动态规划分析失效，需要新的凸性理论与求解框架。

Method: 1) 建立有限时域随机动态规划模型，员工流失服从参数依赖招聘后规模的二项分布；2) 证明变量单期成本的离散凸性及期望总成本的K-凸性；3) 引入二项K-凸性（Binomial-K-convexity）概念，证明其在Bellman函数中出现名传播下的保持性；4) 推导最优政策的(s,S)型结构；5) 开发分段线性逼近方法，转化为MILP求解各期(s,S)参数。

Result: 证明了最优招聘策略具有(s,S)型结构：当员工规模低于阈值s时，招聘至S水平；否则不招聘。基于分段逼近的MILP方法能够快速求解各期最优(s,S)参数，数值实验表明该方法计算效率高且最优性间隙小。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上建立了具有决策依赖二项转移概率的劳动力规划问题的最优策略结构（(s,S)型），并提供了一个实用的混合整数线性规划求解方案，兼具理论严谨性与计算可行性。

Abstract: We study a finite-horizon workforce planning problem in which staff turnover in each period follows a binomial distribution whose parameters depend on the post-hiring workforce level. The model incorporates a fixed hiring cost that is incurred whenever recruitment occurs, regardless of the number of employees hired. The objective is to minimise the expected total cost, including recruitment, salary, and shortage costs, where deviations below period-specific staffing requirements are penalised. To analyse this stochastic dynamic programme with decision-dependent transition probabilities, we establish the discrete convexity of the variable single-period cost (the sum of expected salary and penalty costs) and the K-convexity of the expected total cost. Specifically, we introduce the concept of Binomial-K-convexity to facilitate the proof that K-convexity is preserved under Binomial propagation in the Bellman function. We then show that the optimal hiring policy exhibits an (s, S)-type structure: when the workforce level in a given period falls below a threshold s, staff are hired up to level S; otherwise, no hiring occurs. Furthermore, we develop a piecewise approximation approach that yields a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulation for solving the problem and computing the (s, S) parameters for each period. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves fast computation with small optimality gaps.

</details>


### [150] [No-gap second-order conditions for optimization problems involving transport distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28264)
*Nicolas Borchard,Christian Meyer,Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了度量空间中的优化问题，以传输距离作为正则化项。利用弱-*二阶次导数理论推导无间隙型二阶最优性条件，并证明了与二次增长的等价性，最后将结果应用于度量空间中的最优控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在度量空间中的优化问题中，带有传输距离正则化项的目标函数需要二阶最优性条件来刻画最优解的局部性质，但传统的二阶导数理论在此类非光滑问题中不适用，需要发展弱-*二阶次导数理论。

Method: 采用弱-*二阶次导数理论推导无间隙型（no-gap type）二阶最优性条件；计算弱-*二阶次导数并证明弱-*上/下微分的存在性（epi-differentiability）；在目标函数光滑部分和Kantorovich势（对偶传输问题的解）上施加额外假设，建立与二次增长的等价性。

Result: 获得了无间隙型二阶最优性条件，证明了该条件与二次增长在适当假设下等价；给出了弱-*二阶次导数的具体计算公式，并证明了弱-*epi可微性；成功将上述理论应用于度量空间中的最优控制问题。

Conclusion: 本文建立了度量空间中带传输距离正则化优化问题的完整二阶变分分析框架，通过弱-*二阶次导数实现了必要性条件与充分性条件的无间隙匹配，并为最优控制问题提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We consider optimization problems in the space of measures. As a regularization term, the problem includes the transport distance to a given prior measure. For the derivation of second-order optimality conditions of no-gap type, the theory of weak-$\star$ second subderivatives is used which will lead to an equivalence with quadratic growth under additional assumptions on the smooth part of the objective and on the Kantorovich potential, i.e., the solution of the dual transport problem. Further, the weak-$\star$ second subderivative is calculated and weak-$\star$ epidifferentiability is proven. Finally, the results are applied to optimal control problems in measure space.

</details>


### [151] [Exact and Approximate Solvability of Systems Involving Proximity Operators in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28343)
*Mark Allien D. Roble*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究实Hilbert空间中有限凸函数族近端算子系统的精确解存在性与唯一性，将经典投影问题推广为近端算子方程组，建立近似解存在的充要条件，并引入逆近端性质（IPP）作为逆最佳逼近性质（IBAP）的推广，应用于可行性问题和信号恢复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的投影问题（即寻找满足投影算子方程的点）在优化与逆问题中已有广泛研究，但近端算子作为投影算子的自然推广，其对应的系统方程（proximity operator systems）尚未得到充分的系统研究。本文旨在建立此类系统的解存在性理论，并推广逆最佳逼近性质（IBAP）到更一般的近端算子框架。

Method: 本文首先将有限个凸函数的近端算子方程组作为投影问题的推广进行形式化建模；接着利用凸分析与变分分析的工具建立解存在性与唯一性的充要条件；然后引入逆近端性质（IPP）并给出多种刻画；最后将所获理论结果应用于可行性问题和信号恢复问题以验证框架的有效性。

Result: 建立了近端算子系统精确解的充要条件，给出了近似解存在的充要条件；引入并刻画了逆近端性质（IPP）作为逆最佳逼近性质（IBAP）的推广；在可行性问题和信号恢复问题中展示了所提框架的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 该工作为Hilbert空间中近端算子系统的求解提供了完整的理论框架，将经典投影问题与IBAP理论统一推广到更一般的近端算子情形，为优化和逆问题领域提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: Let $\HH$ be a real Hilbert space and let $(f_i)_{i\in I}$ be a finite family of proper, lower semicontinuous, and convex functions on $\HH$. This study investigates the existence and uniqueness of exact solutions to systems involving proximity operators of the form: \
  $(\forall i\in I)\ \pr{f_i}(x)=p_i,$
  where $(p_i)_{i\in I}$ is a prescribed collection of proximal points in $\HH$, which naturally generalize classical projection problems. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of approximate solutions to such systems. Moreover, we introduce and derive several characterizations of the inverse proximal property (IPP), as a generalization of the inverse best approximation property (IBAP). Applications of the obtained results are presented in the contexts of a feasibility problem and signal recovery problem, demonstrating the relevance of the proposed framework to optimization and inverse problems in Hilbert spaces.

</details>


### [152] [Beyond Hand-Derived Inequalities: Decision Diagrams for Cut Generation in Binary Polynomial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28511)
*Martin Cooper,Margarita Castro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于决策图（DD）的割平面生成方法用于二元多项式优化（BPO）。对于任意选定的支撑集，该方法在局部多重线性多面体中分离出定义面的割，并将其提升回原问题；利用基于递归公式的新型紧凑DD编码（仅显式表示顶点变量，隐式编码超边变量），同时获得多重线性多面体的扩展公式、基于有效反链的宽度刻画（揭示了一类新的多项式可解超图），以及所生成割的面性的证书。实验表明该方法在根节点处以更少的割实现更大的对偶间隙闭合，并显著加速分支定界过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的多线性集强不等式（如two-links、flowers、odd β-cycles）是针对固定支撑模式手工推导的，缺乏通用性和系统性。作者希望提出一种自动化的、适用于任意支撑集的分割方法，避免手工设计不等式。

Method: 提出决策图（DD）方法进行割平面生成。核心包括：(1) 一种基于递归公式的新型紧凑DD编码，仅表示顶点变量，通过状态表示隐式编码超边变量；(2) 从该编码推导出局部多重线性多面体的扩展公式；(3) 通过有效反链进行宽度刻画，揭示多项式可解的超图类别；(4) 提供所生成割的面性（facetness）证书；(5) 探索三种支撑策略：重用（reuse）、扩展（expand）和分区（partition）局部结构为section超图。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的DD方法在根节点处以更少的割数实现更大的对偶间隙闭合（gap closure），且显著加速了分支定界（branch-and-bound）过程，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 决策图方法为BPO的割平面生成提供了一种通用且自动化的框架，能够替代手工推导的不等式，同时在理论和计算两方面均优于现有方法，并开辟了新的多项式可解超图类别。

Abstract: We study cutting-plane generation for binary polynomial optimization (BPO), whose feasible region is the multilinear set of a hypergraph. Strong inequalities for this set---such as two-links, flowers, and odd $β$-cycles---are classically hand-derived for fixed support patterns. Instead, we propose a decision-diagram (DD) approach: for any chosen support, it separates a facet-defining cut in the local multilinear polytope and lifts it back to the original problem. We utilize a novel compact DD encoding based on a recursive formulation that represents only the vertex variables and implicitly encodes the hyperedge variables inside the state representation. From this DD encoding, we also obtain: (i) an extended formulation of the multilinear polytope, (ii) a width characterization via valid antichains that uncovers a new polynomially solvable class of hypergraphs, and (iii) a certificate for the facetness of the generated cuts. We explore three different support strategies for cut generation that reuse, expand, or partition local structures into section hypergraphs. Our empirical results show that our DD-based methodologies achieve a larger gap closure at the root node with fewer cuts than existing procedures and, in turn, markedly accelerate branch-and-bound procedures.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Estimating Treatment Effects for Depression in Longitudinal Therapy Switching Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27214)
*Xinyu Qin,Martin Katzman,Alexandria Greifenberger,Elssa Toumeh,Sachinthya Lokuge,Tia Sternat,Ruiheng Yu,Lu Wang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用专有的纵向重度抑郁症（MDD）临床试验数据，构建下一访视反事实预测任务来估计不同治疗方案下的HAMD-17评分，基准测试了8种估计器（meta-learners、残差法和树方法），发现Causal Forest（CF）表现最优。分析表明症状获益集中在特定的换药方向上，增加剂量通常有益，但存在低强度方案优于高强度方案的例外，且调整混杂后的估计值揭示了适度可操作的真实效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症治疗常因应答不足或不良反应需要换药，但在此场景下估计个体化治疗效果十分困难，因为治疗分配受患者特征混杂、换药引入时变选择偏差，且反事实结局在随访数据中不可观测。现有方法难以在这种复杂临床场景下提供可靠的个体化疗效估计，亟需系统评估不同因果推断估计器的表现。

Method: 使用专有的纵向MDD临床试验数据集，构建下一访视反事实预测任务，以HAMD-17总评分作为结局指标估计不同治疗方案下的反事实结果。基准测试了8种估计器，涵盖meta-learners、基于残差的方法和基于树的方法，通过多个标准评估各估计器的一致性和表现。

Result: Causal Forest（CF）在所有评估标准上展现出最优且最一致的性能。症状获益集中在特定换药方向上，剂量强化总体上是有益的。同时识别出一个反直觉的例外：对于特定患者子集，低强度方案优于高强度替代方案。粗略的观察性比较严重高估了疗效改善，而经混杂调整后的估计值呈现出适度且可操作的效应幅度。

Conclusion: 研究提供了可前瞻性检验的临床决策支持候选方案，可用于抑郁症诊疗。Causal Forest作为可靠的个体化治疗效果估计工具，结合混杂调整后的效应估计，有望为临床医生在抑郁症换药决策中提供数据驱动的参考依据。

Abstract: Depression treatment often requires switching medications due to inadequate response or adverse effects. Estimating individualized treatment effects in this setting is challenging because treatment assignment is confounded by patient characteristics, switching induces time-varying selection, and counterfactual outcomes are not observed in follow-up data. Using a proprietary longitudinal major depressive disorder (MDD) clinical trial dataset, we formulate a next-visit counterfactual prediction task to estimate Hamilton Depression Rating Scale (HAMD-17) total scores under alternative treatments. We benchmark 8 estimators, including meta-learners, residual-based methods, and tree-based approaches. Causal Forest (CF) demonstrates the most favorable and consistent performance across all criteria. Our analysis shows that symptom benefits concentrate in specific switch directions, with dose intensification being generally beneficial. Notably, we identify a counterintuitive exception where a lower-intensity regimen outperforms a higher-intensity alternative for specific patient subsets. While crude observational comparisons substantially overstate gains, confounding-adjusted estimates yield modest, actionable magnitudes. These findings provide prospectively testable candidates for clinical decision support in depression care.

</details>


### [154] [Foundation-Model Earth Representations Enable Regional-Scale Forest Aboveground Biomass Monitoring Across the Northeastern United States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27217)
*Shashika Lamahewage,Chandi Witharana*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究评估了谷歌卫星嵌入（GSE，由AlphaEarth基础模型生成）在美国东北部温带森林区域尺度地上生物量（AGB）估算中的应用。结合LiDAR和GSE的模型R²达0.79，利用年度GSE观测通过时间生长调整将训练数据扩充十倍以上，性能提升至R²=0.82且偏差降低70%以上。研究证明基础模型地球表征可捕获与森林生物量相关的生态信息，为缺乏机载LiDAR覆盖的区域提供了可扩展的年度碳监测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域碳监测受限于野外实测清单和机载结构测量的时空稀疏性，亟需可扩展的替代途径来实现大规模生物量监测。地球观测基础模型提供全球一致的时空表征，有望填补这一空白。

Method: 集成年度GSE观测、机载LiDAR和NEFIN连续森林清单数据到机器学习框架中；利用年度GSE观测通过时间生长调整扩充训练数据；采用超参数优化（通过Monte Carlo模拟评估）和空间自相关分析验证模型性能与残差空间依赖性。

Result: LiDAR-GSE组合模型R²=0.79；年度GSE数据扩充训练集十倍以上后R²提升至0.82，模型偏差降低超过70%；集成基础模型表征和结构预测因子显著降低了残差空间自相关；超参数优化使模型性能变异降低27.9%。

Conclusion: 基础模型地球表征能够捕获与森林生物量相关的生态学意义信息，为缺乏完整机载LiDAR覆盖的区域提供了可扩展的年度碳监测框架，开启了基于全球可用基础模型地球观测的下一代森林碳评估路径。

Abstract: Forest aboveground biomass (AGB) is a critical indicator of ecosystem productivity and terrestrial carbon storage, yet regional carbon monitoring remains constrained by the sparse spatial and temporal availability of field inventories and airborne structural measurements. Recent Earth observation foundation models provide globally consistent geospatial representations derived from diverse multimodal datasets, offering a potential pathway toward scalable biomass monitoring. Here, we evaluate Google Satellite Embeddings (GSE), generated by the AplphaEarth Foundation Model, for regional-scale AGB estimation across diverse temperate forest ecosystems in the northeastern United States. We integrated annual GSE observations, airborne LiDAR, and continuous forest inventory measurements from the Northeastern Forest Inventory Network (NEFIN) within a machine-learning framework. Combined LiDAR-GSE models achieved an R^2 of 0.79 for AGB estimation. Capitalizing on annual GSE observations expanded the training dataset by more than tenfold through temporal growth adjustment, increasing predictive performance to R^2 = 0.82 while reducing model bias by over 70%. Spatial autocorrelation analyses showed that integrating foundation-model representations and structural predictors substantially reduced residual spatial dependence. Monte Carlo simulations demonstrated that hyperparameter optimization reduced model-performance variability by 27.9%. Our findings demonstrate that foundation-model Earth representations capture ecologically meaningful information relevant to forest biomass and provide a scalable framework for annual carbon monitoring in regions with incomplete airborne LiDAR coverage. Our fundings establish a pathway toward next-generation forest carbon assessment based on globally available foundation-model Earth observations.

</details>


### [155] [Psych-ECA: A Reproducible Semi-Synthetic Benchmark for Synthetic Control Arms in Longitudinal Psychiatry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27224)
*Aakash Bhagat,Shashank Choudhary*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文发布Psych-ECA，一个用于精神科药物研发中外部/合成对照臂评估的可复现半合成基准生成器，涵盖了抑郁症（PHQ-9）、焦虑症（HAM-A）和精神病（PANSS）的纵向症状轨迹，并基准测试了八种估计器。研究发现轨迹与灵活机器学习方法具备最佳反事实准确性；Scribe方法在准确性和校准性上均表现最优（90%预测区间经验覆盖率达93-96%）；逆强度校正可减少信息性采样偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部和合成对照臂正在进入精神科药物研发，但该领域缺乏一个从监管者关注的角度评估这些方法的基准：既包括重建未治疗轨迹的准确性，还包括不确定性校准、对精神健康记录中常见的信息性观测时间的稳健性，以及go/no-go试验决策中的假阳性率。真实精神科试验数据需要认证访问且缺乏真实反事实真值，因此需要可复现的半合成基准。

Method: 遵循因果推断中成熟的半合成基准范式（IHDP、ACIC和PK-PD肿瘤生长模拟器），构建Psych-ECA生成器，生成具有已知反事实对照臂、信息性就诊和非独立同分布测量噪声的纵向症状轨迹（PHQ-9、HAM-A、PANSS）。基准测试八种估计器：携带前向填充、合并真实世界数据平均值、最近邻匹配、线性混合模型、梯度提升和Scribe轨迹桥接方法，评估反事实准确性、校准性、对信息性采样稳健性及试验决策假阳性率。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）轨迹和灵活机器学习方法达到最佳反事实准确性（约2.3 PHQ-9 RMSE），优于横截面基线；（2）仅Scribe同时具备准确性和校准性，90%预测区间的经验覆盖率达93-96%，而梯度提升为87-88%，未校准的SDE模型仅62-75%；（3）逆强度校正降低信息性采样下的偏差，而Scribe的校准区间是唯一在信息性程度增加时维持名义假阳性率的轨迹方法。

Conclusion: Psych-ECA为精神科外部对照臂评估提供了一个完全可复现的基准框架，所有代码、数据生成脚本和随机种子均已发布。该基准揭示了一个关键发现：在精神科药物开发中，不仅需要准确的轨迹重建，更需要校准良好的不确定性估计，而这正是当前多种方法所欠缺的。

Abstract: External and synthetic control arms (ECAs) are entering psychiatric drug development, but the field lacks a benchmark that evaluates the properties regulators care about: not only how accurately a method reconstructs untreated trajectories, but whether its uncertainty is calibrated, whether it is robust to the informative observation times common in mental-health records (sicker patients are seen more often), and what false-positive rate it induces in go/no-go trial decisions. Real psychiatric trial data (e.g. STAR-D and registry cohorts) require credentialed access and lack ground-truth counterfactuals, so, following established semi-synthetic benchmarks in causal inference (IHDP, ACIC, and the PK-PD tumor-growth simulator), we release Psych-ECA, a fully reproducible generator of longitudinal symptom trajectories for depression (PHQ-9), anxiety (HAM-A), and psychosis (PANSS) with known counterfactual control arms, informative visits, and validated-scale measurement noise. We benchmark eight estimators spanning carry-forward, pooled real-world-data averages, nearest-neighbour matching, linear mixed models, gradient boosting, and the Scribe trajectory-bridge method. Three findings emerge. First, trajectory and flexible machine learning methods achieve the best counterfactual accuracy (about 2.3 PHQ-9 RMSE), outperforming cross-sectional baselines. Second, only Scribe is both accurate and calibrated, achieving 93-96% empirical coverage of nominal 90% prediction intervals, compared with 87-88% for gradient boosting and 62-75% for uncalibrated SDE models. Third, inverse-intensity correction reduces bias under informative sampling, while Scribe's calibrated intervals are the only trajectory method that maintains nominal false-positive rates as informativeness increases. We release all code, data-generation scripts, and random seeds to enable fully reproducible evaluation.

</details>


### [156] [Who Moved? Ecological Estimates of Turnout & Coalition Support by Ethnicity and Age in Johor, Malaysia, 2022-2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27864)
*Thevesh Thevananthan,Ong Kian Ming*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用马来西亚2026年柔佛州选举的选区人口结构与投票站年龄构成等生态数据，估计不同族裔与年龄群体的投票率和联盟支持率，识别出BN在2026年大胜的五大关键发现，并首次为马来西亚选举提供完全可复现的生态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026年柔佛州选举中BN得票近乎翻倍，但官方数据无法直接揭示按族裔与年龄细分的行为变化。文章旨在通过生态推断方法，拆解投票率与选民转向对选举结果的影响，并弥补马来西亚选举研究中缺乏开放、可复现生态估计的空白。

Method: 利用投票站族裔构成的地区变异以及投票流年龄构成由行政因素导致的变异，构建生态推断模型，对2022年以来柔佛州三次选举中的投票率和联盟支持率按族裔与年龄进行估计，并对估计值进行针对设定目标（estimand）的留出验证误差检验。

Result: 五个核心发现：（1）投票率是结果的首要驱动因素，族裔内跨选举变化最高达26个百分点并遵循生命周期曲线；（2）BN的激增几乎与PN在马来选民中的崩溃一一对应，且不受PN在56席中23席弃选的影响；（3）向BN的最大反转发生在年轻马来选民中，他们曾推动2022年"绿色浪潮"；（4）PH在华人选民中的下滑三分之二源于华人投票率下降，对BN的跨党转向较小；（5）2026年BN支持率表面上存在年龄梯度，但主要来自族裔构成差异而非组内行为差异，到2026年马来选民对各联盟的支持率在年龄上基本持平。

Conclusion: 投票率变动（尤其族裔与生命周期差异）是理解2026年柔佛州选举格局转变的关键；本研究提供了首个开放且完全可复现的马来西亚选举生态估计，并引入针对目标量的验证误差度量，为后续研究奠定方法论基础。

Abstract: At the July 2026 Johor state election, Barisan Nasional (BN) swept the state and nearly doubled its vote relative to 2022. We exploit geographic variation in the ethnic composition of polling stations and administration-induced variation in the age composition of polling streams to estimate turnout and coalition support by ethnicity and age for the three elections involving Johor since 2022, with five key findings. (1) Turnout is a first-order driver of outcomes, moving by up to 26 points within an ethnic group across elections and following a life-cycle profile. (2) BN's surge was a near one-for-one Perikatan Nasional (PN) collapse among Malay voters, robust to PN's stand-down in 23 of 56 seats. (3) The largest reversal towards BN was among young Malays, who had previously carried the `green wave' of 2022. (4) Two-thirds of Pakatan Harapan's (PH) decline among the Chinese electorate came through lower Chinese turnout; any crossover to BN was comparatively small. (5) The apparent youth gradient in BN's 2026 support is largely ethnic composition rather than within-group behaviour: by 2026, Malay support for every coalition was close to flat in age. More broadly, we provide what we believe are the first open and fully reproducible ecological estimates of voting behaviour for any Malaysian election, accompanied by estimand-specific validation errors measured against held-out behaviour.

</details>


### [157] [What exam scores can and cannot prove about unauthorized AI assistance: Evidence from a highly public classroom episode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27978)
*Chad M. Topaz,Utsav Bahl*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文分析了一所大学某课程中试成绩从95.7骤降至48.8的事件，通过排列检验和模型比较证明期中与期末成绩间无统计学可检测关联，为评估生成式AI使用争议提供了统计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极高分期中考试后，期末考试成绩大幅下滑被归因于期中考试中未经授权的生成式AI使用，但存在多种替代解释（考试焦虑、期末更难、退课、均值回归）。研究者希望用统计分析来判断这些解释中哪一项最能得到数据支持。

Method: 采用排列检验评估期中与期末成绩之间的关联性；进行模型复杂度校正后的模型比较；在模拟课堂中检验不同解释下两种数据模式（相关系数仅0.06、个人变化幅度达-100到+4）同时出现的频率。

Result: 排列检验未发现学生期中与期末成绩间存在统计上可检测的关联，而替代解释所预测的关联强度在90%以上的情况下可被检测到；考虑模型复杂度后，期中成绩不携带期末信息的模型与允许存在关联的模型拟合效果相当。两种模式在模拟中仅当学生自身能力纽带被切断时才会规律性地同时出现。

Conclusion: 数据并不要求期中与期末成绩之间存在关联，多种机制（如期中非本人作答或期末考察完全不同内容）都可能导致纽带断裂。分数无法区分这些可能性，也无法识别哪些学生作弊。该研究为未来生成式AI学术评估争议提供了统计证据评估框架。

Abstract: In spring 2026, an economics professor at Brown University gave a take-home midterm and, after unusually high scores, made the final exam proctored. Among the 59 students who completed the course, average scores fell from 95.7 out of 100 to 48.8. The instructor attributed the drop to unauthorized use of generative AI on the midterm; others proposed test anxiety, a harder final, student withdrawals, and regression to the mean. The publicly released scores, one midterm-final pair per student, show two striking patterns: the correlation between a student's two scores is only 0.06, and individual changes range from a 4-point gain to a 100-point loss. Permutation tests find no statistically detectable association between students' midterm and final scores, yet would detect association of the strength the alternative explanations predict more than 90 percent of the time. Once model complexity is accounted for, a model in which a student's midterm carries no information about that student's final describes the data as well as any model that permits an association. Neither result rules out a weak association, but together they show that the data do not require any. In simulated classes where each student's two scores remain linked through that student's own proficiency, as the alternative explanations imply, the two patterns almost never appear together; they appear together regularly only when that link is nearly severed. More than one mechanism could have severed it: midterm answers that were not the students' own work would have done so, and so would a final testing substantially different material, with no assistance involved. The scores cannot distinguish these possibilities or identify which students, if any, used unauthorized assistance. Our study offers a framework for evaluating statistical evidence in future disputes over generative AI use in academic assessment.

</details>


### [158] [Bridging Probabilistic LLMs and Deterministic Statistical Validation: The PROVE Multi-Agent Framework for Clinical Trial Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28218)
*Zhaohua Lu,Cheng Zheng,Yuanyuan Han*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出PROVE（Programmatic Reporting and Output Verification Engine），一个用于临床试验TFL（表格、图形和列表）输出验证的可审计框架。该框架结合LLM用于表格语义解释（可选启用），同时将数值和逻辑判定保留给程序化验证器。在10组复制合成肿瘤学报告包（每组注入15个随机差异）的实验中，PROVE在精确标签设置下达到完美分类；在标签变体设置中，LLM辅助语义匹配将整体召回率从0.588提升至0.993，F1从0.735提升至0.996。结论是LLM最适合解释TFL措辞和格式的真实世界变体，而可执行检查应负责最终数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验TFL（表格、图表和列表）的准确性和一致性在监管报告中仍是重大挑战。虽然独立编程和人工审查是必要的质控实践，但跨输出验证仍高度依赖审查人员目测，可能遗漏结构、逻辑或算术差异。LLM虽能帮助解释变化多样的表格语言和导航冗长研究文档，但不能可靠替代程序化统计检查。因此需要一个结合LLM语义解释能力与程序化数值验证优势的混合框架。

Method: PROVE框架的核心设计是：使用可选的LLM和检索增强支持进行表格解释，同时将数值和逻辑判定保留给程序化验证器。它链接发现结果到源证据，支持跨输出一致性检查，并允许根据研究需求启用或禁用LLM。实验设计为：生成10组复制的合成肿瘤学报告包（从原始数据经SDTM、ADaM到TFL输出），每组包含配对的标准包和注入差异包（每个注入15个随机差异），并在两种表格标签设置下评估：精确标签匹配验证器词汇，以及语义相近但措辞不同的标签。比较了LLM辅助语义匹配与精确匹配、模糊词汇匹配和嵌入相似性变体。

Result: 在精确标签设置下，所有自动化PROVE变体在实现的规则类别内达到完美分类。在标签变体设置中，LLM辅助语义匹配显著优于其他方法：整体召回率从0.588提升至0.993，整体F1从0.735提升至0.996（对比精确匹配、模糊词汇和嵌入相似性变体）。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，LLM在解释TFL措辞和格式的真实世界变体方面最有价值，而可执行检查应始终负责最终数值验证。PROVE这种"LLM辅助解释+程序化验证"的混合架构能够在保持可审计性和可靠性的同时，有效处理临床报告中的语言变异。

Abstract: Ensuring the accuracy and consistency of clinical trial Tables, Figures, and Listings (TFLs) remains a major challenge in regulatory reporting. Independent programming and manual review are essential quality-control practices, but cross-output verification still depends heavily on reviewer inspection and may miss structural, logical, or arithmetic discrepancies. Large language models (LLMs) can help interpret varied table language and navigate lengthy study documents, but they are not reliable substitutes for programmed statistical checks. We introduce PROVE (Programmatic Reporting and Output Verification Engine), an auditable framework that uses optional LLM and retrieval support for table interpretation while reserving numerical and logical decisions for programmed validators. PROVE links findings to source evidence, supports cross-output consistency checks, and allows LLM use to be enabled or disabled based on study requirements. We evaluated PROVE using ten replicated synthetic oncology reporting packages generated from raw data through SDTM, ADaM, and TFL outputs, with paired clean and discrepancy-injected packages; each replicate included 15 randomly injected discrepancies. We examined two table-label settings: exact labels matching the validator vocabulary and labels with similar clinical meaning but different wording. Within the implemented rule classes, all automated PROVE variants achieved perfect classification in the exact-label setting. In the label-variation setting, LLM-assisted semantic matching improved overall recall from 0.588 to 0.993 and overall F1 from 0.735 to 0.996 compared with exact-match, fuzzy lexical, and embedding-similarity variants. These findings suggest that LLMs are most useful for interpreting real-world variation in TFL wording and formatting, while executable checks should remain responsible for final numerical validation.

</details>


### [159] [Snapshot plots: displaying summary tables as parallel univariate plots with consistent color highlighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28302)
*Matthias Schonlau,Sandra Huang,Tiancheng Yang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出"快照图"(snapshot plots)，一种用于可视化医学和社会科学研究中"Table 1"（样本背景特征汇总表）的并行单变量图方法，通过一致性高亮设计来促进组间背景特征的比较，并提供网页应用和Python实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的"Table 1"以表格形式呈现样本背景特征的分组汇总统计，虽然在实证研究中被广泛使用，但难以直观地促进组间比较，且对数值型变量的细节展示有限。作者希望通过可视化手段提升此类表格的可读性和比较效率。

Method: 快照图本质上是对吊床图(hammock plots，一种适用于混合分类/数值数据的平行坐标图)的边角情况（edge case）处理。它采用并行的单变量图（parallel univariate plots），并以一致性的高亮方式（consistent highlighting）呈现各组数据，从而支持组间背景特征的直观比较。作者还提供了Web应用和Python实现。

Result: 作者在两个真实的"Table 1"数据集上演示了快照图的有效性，展示了其相较于传统表格在组间比较和数值变量细节呈现方面的优势。

Conclusion: 快照图是对传统"Table 1"的有力可视化补充，能够帮助研究者在处理组间比较时更直观、更高效地洞察背景特征的差异。

Abstract: For empirical studies, social and health scientists give background characteristics of their sample and summarize them in the famous "Table 1". When treatment/ control groups are present, this table gives summary statistics by group to see whether the background characteristics differ by group. We propose snapshot plots --- parallel univariate plots with consistent highlighting --- to visualize such tables. Compared to "Table 1", such plots are designed to facilitate comparisons of background characteristics --- in particular among groups --- and give more detail on numerical variables. We provide a web app as well as a python implementation of snapshot plots. Snapshot plots arise as edge cases of hammock plots (parallel coordinate plots for mixed categorical/ numerical data). We demonstrate the usefulness of snapshot plots for two "Table 1"s.

</details>


### [160] [Stated, Realized, and Optimal Aiming Strategies for the Tennis Serve: Experimental Evidence from Collegiate Athletes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28500)
*Nathan Sandholtz,Ryan Hanson,Ron Hager,Stephanie Kovalchik,Gilbert Fellingham*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了网球发球的最佳瞄准位置，通过在BYU男子网球队进行实验收集发球落点数据，结合贝叶斯两期马尔可夫决策过程估计发球分布与最佳目标。研究发现最佳二发比一发更保守，且球员实际发球中心比其声称目标更保守并接近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网球发球存在瞄准边界与非边界之间的权衡：瞄准靠近边界使回球更困难但增加失误概率。由于普通比赛数据无法观测球员的意图目标，导致难以估计球员的执行误差，从而无法准确评估最优瞄准策略。

Method: 1) 与BYU男子网球队成员合作进行实验，球员标注自认为最优的目标位置并反复发球，记录精确落点；2) 估计考虑被网触截断的球员特定发球分布；3) 结合点获胜奖励曲面，构建具有连续空间动作空间的贝叶斯两期马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）来求解最优瞄准策略。

Result: 1) 在每一个球员-区域组合中，估计的最优二发目标都比一发更保守，与既有理论一致；2) 球员声称的目标总体比最优更激进，但其实际发球中心比声称目标更保守，且显著更接近估计的最优点。

Conclusion: 球员的发球实际行为比其主观声称的目标更接近最优策略，说明现实发球行为可能比自报目标更具理性；同时验证了二发应更保守的理论预测，为发球瞄准策略提供了实证依据。

Abstract: We study where, within a chosen Wide or T region, a tennis player should aim a serve. Aiming near a service-box boundary makes a serve harder to return but raises the probability of a fault, creating a trade-off that depends on the player's execution error. Ordinary match data provide limited information about execution error because players' intended targets are unobserved. We therefore conducted an experiment with members of the Brigham Young University men's tennis team. Each player identified targets they believed to be optimal, which we marked on the court. Players then served repeatedly toward each target while we recorded precise bounce locations. From these data, we estimate player-specific serve distributions while accounting for serves censored by net contact, then combine them with an estimated point-win reward surface in a Bayesian, two-period Markov decision process with a continuous spatial action space. We find that the estimated optimal second-serve target is more conservative than its first-serve counterpart in every player-region combination, consistent with prior theory. Also, while players' stated targets were generally more aggressive than optimal, their realized serve centers were more conservative than those targets and markedly closer to the estimated optima.

</details>


### [161] [ABC methods for IoT Emitter Geolocalisation using LEO Satellite Doppler Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28585)
*B. Ristic,Y. Choi,D. Y. Kim,A. Hourani*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究利用低地球轨道（LEO）卫星在观测窗口内采集的多普勒频率测量数据，对静止地面物联网（IoT）无线电发射器进行被动定位。由于低成本IoT设备的无线电发射受多种复合测量误差影响，似然函数无法以封闭形式表达，因此采用近似贝叶斯计算（ABC）方法来解决该问题，并通过数值实验评估了两种ABC方法（拒绝采样ABC和序贯蒙特卡洛ABC）的统计与计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT设备发射的无线电信号受多种复合测量误差影响，导致似然函数无法以闭合形式解析求解，传统定位方法难以直接应用，需要开发不依赖显式似然函数的定位方法。

Method: 采用近似贝叶斯计算（ABC）框架进行被动定位，具体比较了两种ABC变体：拒绝采样ABC和序贯蒙特卡洛ABC。ABC方法通过模拟观测数据并与之比较，绕过难以处理的似然函数进行后验推断。

Result: 数值结果展示了两种ABC方法在被动定位任务中的统计性能与计算性能，验证了ABC框架在该问题上的可行性，并比较了两种算法的收敛效率与精度。

Conclusion: ABC方法为LEO卫星多普勒被动定位中似然函数不可解析的问题提供了有效解决方案，两种ABC方法均能完成任务，且在计算成本与精度之间呈现不同权衡。

Abstract: We address the problem of passive localisation of a stationary, ground-level IoT radio emitter using Doppler frequency measurements collected by low-Earth orbit (LEO) satellites during an observation window. The problem is challenging because radio emission from low-cost IoT devices is affected by various compounding sources of measurement error, that collectively render the likelihood function intractable in a closed form. Hence, we apply and investigate the performance of Approximate Bayesian Computation (ABC) methods for this task. Numerical results demonstrate the statistical and computational performance of two ABC methods, rejection sampling ABC and sequential Monte Carlo ABC.

</details>
